### [1] [Unified Backbone Refinement for Diffusion Models via Internal-Latent Analysis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.09753)
*Haksoo Lim,Myeongjin Lee,Wonjoon Chang,Jaesik Choi*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出DUNE，一种无需训练的扩散模型优化框架，通过检测并抑制深度潜在表示中的异常波动来提升生成质量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 扩散模型性能与去噪骨干网络密切相关，但现有方法对扩散组件的阶段性行为缺乏系统分析，且生成结果中常出现伪影。

Method: 提出DUNE框架，基于EMA标准检测深度低噪声内部潜在表示中的突变，并对检测到的条目施加骨干网络特定的抑制操作。该检测-抑制原则可同时适用于U-Net和Transformer架构。

Result: 在多种骨干网络上的大量实验表明，DUNE在提升生成保真度的同时减少了幻觉现象。

Conclusion: 本文揭示了扩散骨干网络应该在何时何地受到控制，为扩散模型的优化提供了新视角。

Abstract: Diffusion models have achieved remarkable success across diverse domains, with performance closely related to the denoising backbones that parameterize the score function. In this paper, we present a systematic, phase-aware analysis of diffusion components and show that abrupt, early-stage fluctuations in deep latents are strongly associated with artifacts. Guided by these findings, we introduce DUNE (Diffusion Unified Network refiNEr), a training-free refinement framework that detects abrupt deviations in deep low-noise internal latents using a shared EMA-based criterion, and applies backbone-specific suppression to the detector-selected entries. Although derived from U-Net, the same detect-suppress principle extends naturally to Transformer-based diffusion models by acting on the latents of deep self-attention blocks. Extensive experiments across multiple backbones indicate that DUNE improves fidelity while reducing hallucinations, offering new insight into where and when diffusion backbones should be controlled.

</details>


### [2] [Cross-Subject Modeling for Widefield Calcium Imaging via Atlas-Aligned Spatiotemporal Tokenization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.09754)
*Mohammad Hosseini,Eray Erturk,Saba Hashemi,Maryam M. Shanechi*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: WiCAT是一个多主体宽场钙成像预训练模型，通过自监督学习和图谱标记化，实现了跨主体、任务和数据集的零样本行为解码。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 宽场钙成像数据维度高、结构复杂且存在大量任务无关活动，现有模型多局限于单会话分析，缺乏可扩展性和泛化能力，且多主体模型的零样本行为解码尚未实现。

Method: 提出WiCAT模型，采用基于图谱的标记化方案，无需会话特定组件，通过自监督预训练学习全局共享的时空表征。

Result: 在多个宽场数据集上，WiCAT优于单会话基线模型，支持轻量级下游解码，实现跨主体、任务和数据集的迁移，并在未见主体上实现鲁棒的零样本连续行为解码和脑区重建。

Conclusion: WiCAT作为宽场数据基础建模的第一步，证明了多主体自监督预训练在宽场钙成像中的有效性，为零样本行为解码开辟了新路径。

Abstract: Large-scale, multi-subject widefield calcium imaging provides unprecedented access to brain-wide cortical dynamics. However, the high dimensionality, complex spatiotemporal structure, and substantial task-irrelevant activity in widefield recordings have largely restricted modeling efforts to single-session analyses, limiting scalability and generalization. While multi-subject pretrained models have been explored for some neural modalities, multi-subject models for widefield calcium imaging have not yet been demonstrated; further, subject-invariant zero-shot behavior decoding remains elusive for multi-subject models across neural modalities more broadly. As a first step toward foundation modeling of widefield data, we introduce WiCAT, a multi-subject model that leverages self-supervised pretraining to both outperform single-session models and enable zero-shot behavior decoding on unseen subjects. WiCAT introduces an atlas-grounded tokenization scheme without session-specific components and learns globally shared spatiotemporal representations. Across multiple widefield datasets, the pretrained model supports lightweight downstream decoding, transfers across subjects, tasks, and datasets, and outperforms baseline models. Notably, the model also achieves robust zero-shot continuous behavior decoding and left-out brain region reconstruction on unseen subjects.

</details>


### [3] [RSLoRA: Training-free Rank Allocation for LoRA via Representational Sensitivity Probing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.09757)
*Jiaqi Liu,Haidong Kang,Qihui Zhao,Guo Yu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: RSLoRA提出了一种无需训练和梯度的秩分配方法，通过激活空间几何分析来动态确定各层所需秩大小，在多个基准上超越现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LoRA方法统一分配秩忽略了不同层的功能异质性，而现有秩分配方法要么计算成本高，要么无法捕捉任务特定的表示流形。

Method: 提出RSLoRA，通过虚拟表示探测机制，向模型注入结构化低秩噪声，利用Effective Rank和Frechet Distance衡量流形位移，从而识别高敏感性模块并分配更高秩。

Result: 在多个主流基准上，RSLoRA持续优于AdaLoRA、GoRA等最先进的秩分配方法。

Conclusion: RSLoRA提供了一种高效、鲁棒且具有表示感知能力的解决方案，无需迭代训练调整和反向梯度，适用于大规模模型微调。

Abstract: Low-Rank Adaptation (LoRA) has become a cornerstone of parameter-efficient fine-tuning (PEFT); however, the conventional practice of uniform rank assignment ignores the functional heterogeneity of neural layers. Existing rank allocation methods typically struggle with a trade-off between computational intensity and heuristic simplicity: training-based methods suffer from prohibitive overhead, while pre-allocation methods fail to capture the dynamic task-specific representation manifold. In this paper, we propose RSLoRA (Representational Sensitivity LoRA), a training-free and gradient-free rank allocator driven by activation-space geometry. We identify a "sensitivity regime shift" across layers, observing that static weight analysis and local gradients are insufficient to reflect how updates reshape a model's internal representations. To address this, RSLoRA introduces a virtual representational probing mechanism. By simulating adaptation through structured low-rank noise and measuring the resulting manifold displacement by using Effective Rank and Frechet Distance, we identify high-sensitivity modules that require higher rank capacity. Our framework effectively bridges the gap between expert-crafted heuristics and actual representational impact. Extensive evaluations demonstrate that RSLoRA consistently outperforms state-of-the-art allocators (e.g., AdaLoRA, GoRA) across mainstream benchmarks. By eliminating the need for iterative training-time adjustments and backward gradients, RSLoRA provides a highly efficient, robust, and representation-aware solution for large-scale model adaptation.

</details>


### [4] [ReflectWorld-MM: An Entity-Oriented Multi-Media Memory System for Open-Ended Video Streams](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.09759)
*Xiaokang Ma,Yifan Sun,Zhihong Jin,Jie Gu,Yudong Luo,Shenyi Shao,Chu Tang,Jingmin Chen,Li Pu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: ReflectWorld-MM是一个面向实体的多模态记忆系统，专为开放视频流设计，采用分层记忆结构，在六个长视频和终身记忆基准测试中均取得最佳成绩。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有系统将记忆局限于模型上下文或扁平特征存储中，以帧而非持续实体组织记忆，无法有效追踪跨时间重现的人物和事物，限制了在开放视频流上的应用。

Method: 提出三部分架构：1）感知前端，将流视频转化为实体解析的观察；2）分层长期记忆，基于人类记忆理论，耦合多尺度情景记忆、不断演化的实体中心语义记忆和程序记忆；3）完整实现，可接入任意流和现成助手。

Result: 在六个长视频和终身记忆基准测试中，ReflectWorld-MM在所有六个任务上均取得最高准确率，超越强记忆智能体和前沿模型。

Conclusion: 以实体为中心的分层记忆架构有效提升了开放视频流中智能体的持续观察、记忆和推理能力。

Abstract: Building assistants that can continually watch the world, remember what they see, and reason over their accumulated experience is a long-standing goal, and recently multimodal agents equipped with long-term memory over video streams have attracted increasing interest. Unfortunately, existing systems either keep their memory inside the model context or in a flat feature store, and organize it around frames rather than around the persistent entities a stream is really about, which confines them to bounded videos and weakens their ability to track who and what reappears over time. In this paper, we propose ReflectWorld-MM, an entity-oriented multi-media memory system for open-ended video streams. It consists of three parts. The first is a perception front-end that turns a streaming video into entity-resolved observations under a bounded short-term memory. The second is a hierarchical long-term memory, grounded in human memory theory, that couples a multi-scale episodic memory, an evolving entity-centric semantic memory, and a procedural memory. The third is a complete realization, built for real-world operation, that ingests arbitrary streams and plugs into off-the-shelf assistants. Across six long-video and lifelong-memory benchmarks, ReflectWorld-MM achieves the best accuracy on all six, outperforming strong memory agents and a frontier model.

</details>


### [5] [Knowledge-Constrained Shape Optimization with a Mixture-of-Experts Neural Operator for High-Confidence Design](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.09763)
*Wenhao Fan,Yuanwei Bin,Jianghan Gu,Wenfa Luo,Jiao Xiang,Yuntian Chen,Shiyi Chen*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种知识约束的形状优化框架，结合混合专家神经算子（MoE-NO）和不确定性估计策略，在异构空气动力学数据集上实现了高精度阻力预测（MAPE 1.16%）和趋势一致性（94.34%），并经过CFD验证实现了约4%至10%的阻力系数降低。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 工程形状优化面临两大挑战：依赖专家经验的手动设置（如可编辑区域、变形范围等），以及基于代理模型的优化在异构几何数据库和分布外设计上的不可靠性。

Method: 1）提出知识约束的形状优化框架，将知识约束和用户意图转化为DFFD变形算子的可量化参数；2）开发混合专家神经算子（MoE-NO）提升异构空气动力学数据集的阻力预测和趋势一致性；3）基于MoE-NO编码器和马氏距离引入不确定性估计策略，检测分布外几何并选择性触发物理求解器反馈进行局部样本丰富。

Result: 在MPV、SUV、Sedan数据集上，MoE-NO测试集MAPE为1.16%，趋势预测准确率为94.34%，优于最佳基线的1.52%和90.34%；车辆形状优化实验经CFD验证，阻力系数降低约4%至10%。

Conclusion: 所提框架有效解决了工程形状优化中依赖专家经验和代理模型不可靠的问题，实现了工程感知的可控约束优化，并在实际空气动力学设计中取得了显著性能提升。

Abstract: Engineering shape optimization faces challenges in both expert-dependent problem setup and surrogate-model reliability. In practical aerodynamic design, optimization settings such as editable regions, deformation ranges, and design-preservation constraints are typically specified manually by experienced engineers, while surrogate-based optimization may become unreliable for heterogeneous geometry databases and out-of-distribution designs. To address these challenges, we propose a knowledge-constrained shape-optimization framework that translates knowledge-based constraints and user intent into quantifiable parameters of DFFD-based deformation operators, enabling engineering-aware and controllable constrained optimization. We further develop a Mixture-of-Experts Neural Operator (MoE-NO) to improve drag prediction and trend consistency over heterogeneous aerodynamic datasets. Based on the MoE-NO encoder and Mahalanobis distance, an uncertainty-estimation strategy is introduced to detect out-of-distribution geometries and selectively trigger physics-solver feedback for local sample enrichment. Experiments on in-house MPV, SUV, and Sedan datasets show that MoE-NO achieves a test-set MAPE of $1.16\%$ and a trend-prediction accuracy of $94.34\%$, outperforming the best baseline results of $1.52\%$ and $90.34\%$, respectively. Vehicle shape-optimization experiments further yield CFD-validated drag coefficient reductions of approximately $4\%$ to $10\%$.

</details>


### [6] [Low-Power License Plate Detection and Recognition on a RISC-V Multi-Core MCU-Based Vision System](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.09768)
*Lorenzo Lamberti,Manuele Rusci,Marco Fariselli,Francesco Paci,Luca Benini*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文首次在低功耗MCU设备（基于9核RISC-V处理器GAP8）上实现了自动车牌识别（ALPR），采用SSDlite-MobilenetV2进行车牌检测、LPRNet进行字符识别，在117mW功耗下达到1.09 FPS，比树莓派3系统节能73倍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有ALPR系统多依赖高性能计算平台（如树莓派），功耗较高，缺乏在超低功耗MCU级边缘设备上的解决方案。

Method: 基于9核RISC-V处理器GAP8和QVGA超低功耗灰度图像传感器，采用SSDlite-MobilenetV2进行车牌检测、LPRNet进行光学字符识别的多模型推理流水线，并应用压缩与优化策略。

Result: 车牌检测mAP达38.9%，字符识别率>99.13%；在真实数据上可识别小至30x5像素的车牌；多模型推理（687 MMAC）在117mW功耗下达到1.09 FPS；相比树莓派3系统节能73倍。

Conclusion: 首次在MCU级设备上实现如此复杂度的网络推理，且未使用硬连线加速引擎，保持了完全的算法灵活性，为低功耗边缘ALPR提供了可行的解决方案。

Abstract: In this paper, we present the first (to the best of our knowledge) demonstration of a low-power MCU-based edge device for Automatic License Plate Recognition (ALPR). The design leverages on a 9-core RISC-V processor, GAP8, coupled with a QVGA ultra-low-power greyscale imager. The proposed visual processing pipeline uses a multi-model inference approach based on SSDlite-MobilenetV2 for license plate detection and LPRNet for optical character recognition, reaching a 38.9% mAP score for the first task and a recognition rate of >99.13% for the latter on public datasets. On real-world data, the pipeline recognizes registration numbers when the size of LP crops is as small as 30x5 pixels. Thanks to the applied compression and optimization strategies, the multi-model inference (687 MMAC) achieves a throughput of 1.09 FPS at a power cost of 117 mW when running on GAP8. Our solution is the first MCU-class device embedding such a level of network complexity, resulting to be 73x more energy-efficient w.r.t. precedent mobile-class ALPR system featuring a Raspberry Pi3. The proposed design does not resort to any hardwired acceleration engines, thus retaining full flexibility for future algorithmic improvements.

</details>


### [7] [Time Imprint: Learning Time-Aware Representations in Multi-Modal Knowledge Graphs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.09777)
*Pengyu Zhang,Klim Zaporojets,Congfeng Cao,Jia-Hong Huang,Paul Groth*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出Time Imprint框架，将时间作为独立模态与文本、图像对齐，通过三视图对比学习及时间戳选择与聚合设计，有效提升多模态知识图谱中相似实体的区分能力，在链接预测任务上达到SOTA，Hits@1最高提升6.07%，高歧义样本上提升达58%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有MMKG中相似实体的多模态特征难以区分，时间信息作为额外模态可解决该问题，但面临时间语义稀疏和多时间戳引入噪声两大挑战。

Method: 提出Time Imprint框架：将时间作为实体级模态，通过三视图对比学习对齐时间、文本和图像表示；同时研究紧凑时间戳子集选择设计空间，利用注意力池化聚合选定时间戳形成判别性时间嵌入。

Result: 在三个MMKG基准上，链接预测Hits@1最高提升6.07%，高歧义样本（top-1%）上提升达58%，且训练开销适度。

Conclusion: 将时间作为独立模态能有效提升MMKG中实体区分能力，时间戳选择与聚合设计对性能至关重要，该框架在多种融合策略和时间戳质量下均表现出鲁棒性。

Abstract: Multi-Modal Knowledge Graphs (MMKGs) enrich entities with multiple modalities such as text and images, yet entities with highly similar multi-modal features remain difficult to distinguish. Temporal information of an entity can serve as an additional modality to disambiguate such entities, but existing approaches rarely treat time as a separate modality alongside text and images due to two major challenges: (1) sparse temporal semantics, which hinder alignment with richer modalities, and (2) multiple timestamps, which introduce noise or reduce robustness in representation learning. To address these challenges, we propose Time Imprint, a framework that treats time as an entity-level modality and jointly aligns temporal, textual, and visual representations via a three-view contrastive objective. Additionally, to mitigate multi-timestamp ambiguity, Time Imprint studies a compact timestamp subset selection design space and aggregates the selected timestamps into a discriminative temporal embedding with attention pooling, balancing temporal specificity and robustness. Experiments on three MMKG benchmarks demonstrate that Time Imprint achieves state-of-the-art link prediction performance, improving Hits@1 by up to 6.07\% overall and yielding up to 58\% gains on the subset of the top-1\% ambiguity samples. We further examine different fusion strategies and the sensitivity to timestamp availability and quality, clarifying when and why time-as-modality is most beneficial, while adding only modest training overhead. We release our code at https://anonymous.4open.science/r/Time-Imprint.

</details>


### [8] [A Generalized Deep Non-negative Matrix Factorization Approach for SAR Automatic Target Recognition](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.09779)
*Yunhong Zhang,Changjie Cao,Zhongli Zhou,Bingli Liu,Zongjie Cao,Zongyong Cui,Ying Yang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种广义深度非负矩阵分解（G-DNMF）方法，用于解决SAR自动目标识别中深度非负矩阵分解（DNMF）存在的误差累积和局部最优问题。该方法舍弃了逐层分解策略，采用拉格朗日乘子法推导全局最优更新规则，在MSTAR和OpenSARship数据集上表现出更优的稳定性和识别性能，并增强了特征的可解释性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的深度非负矩阵分解（DNMF）方法在从SAR目标样本中提取多层特征时，采用逐层分解策略，容易导致误差累积和局部最优问题，从而使得随着层数增加，识别精度无法持续提升，亟需一种鲁棒的多层特征提取方法。

Method: 提出广义深度非负矩阵分解（G-DNMF），摒弃逐层分解策略，使用拉格朗日乘子法推导各参数的全局最优更新规则，理论上证明了基于编码矩阵和混合矩阵的DNMF方法均为本方法的特例。

Result: 在MSTAR和OpenSARship数据集上，G-DNMF在稳定性和识别性能方面均优于现有DNMF算法及其变体；通过各层特征图像和重构原始图像验证了方法的纯加性多层特征理解能力和可解释性。

Conclusion: G-DNMF有效缓解了局部最优风险和消除了误差累积，显著提升了DNMF的多层特征提取能力，为SAR自动目标识别提供了一种更具可解释性和鲁棒性的特征提取方案。

Abstract: The deep nonnegative matrix factorization (DNMF) technique is proposed to address the low interpretability of deep learning-based methods in extracting multilayer features from synthetic aperture radar (SAR) target samples. However, existing DNMF methods employ a layer-by-layer decomposition strategy, which is prone to causing error accumulation and local optimum, thereby hindering a consistent improvement in recognition accuracy as the number of layer increases. In this paper, a robust multilayer feature extraction method, termed generalized deep non-negative matrix factorization (G-DNMF), is proposed to address the above challenges in SAR automatic target recognition (ATR). The G-DNMF aims global optimality and derives the update rules for each parameter using lagrangian multiplier method. The new update formula indicates that both the DNMF method based on the encoding matrix and the mixing matrix are special cases of the proposed method, theoretically demonstrating the universality of proposed method. In general, the proposed method discards the layer-by-layer decomposition strategy, thereby effectively mitigating the risk of local optima and eliminating error accumulation, leading to a significant improvement in DNMF's multi-layer feature extraction capability. The experimental results, by presenting the feature images extracted from each layer by G-DNMF and the reconstructed original images, verified the proposed method's pure additive understanding of multi-layer features and demonstrated its interpretability. The experimental results based on MSTAR and OpenSARship datasets show that G-DNMF outperforms existing DNMF algorithms and their derivatives in terms of stability and recognition performance.

</details>


### [9] [Towards Real-World Wearable Motion Reconstruction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.09780)
*Andrea Boscolo Camiletto,Rishabh Dabral,Eduardo Alvarado,Thabo Beeler,Marc Habermann,Christian Theobalt*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出WHIP模型，利用多模态可穿戴设备（智能手机、手表、眼镜、鞋垫等）数据，从任意传感器子集重建全身3D运动，并提供了一个包含50种活动的大规模数据集。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有运动捕捉方法依赖固定传感器配置（如IMU服或头显中心设备），无法泛化到不同穿戴设备组合。作者主张应优先使用无感且轻量的日常设备（如手机、手表），研究它们之间的协同性。

Method: 1）构建大规模多模态数据集，同步消费者级传感器与真实3D运动数据；2）提出WHIP生成模型，可从任意可用传感器子集重建运动，鲁棒处理缺失模态，生成物理合理的运动。

Result: WHIP能有效处理缺失模态，在不同传感器组合下生成物理合理的全身运动；数据集覆盖50种日常、体育和社交活动。

Conclusion: 系统研究了不同传感器模态之间的互补性，证明了从任意可穿戴传感器子集进行全身运动捕捉的可行性，提供了基准方法和数据集。

Abstract: The modern-day surge in popularity of wearable devices poses a fundamentally unique motion capture problem: reconstructing full-body movement from any set of sensing hardware worn at a given moment. Yet, most research efforts assume fixed sensor configurations (e.g. IMU suits or HMD-centric rigs) and cannot generalize across them. In contrast, we argue that motion capture should prioritize unobtrusive and lightweight devices such as smartphones, smartwatches, smart glasses, and smart insoles, and study the interplay between them. To this end, we make three contributions. First, we present a large-scale multi-modal dataset synchronizing these consumer-grade sensors with ground-truth 3D motion, spanning 50 diverse activities including everyday tasks, sports, and social interactions. Second, we propose WHIP, a baseline generative model that reconstructs motion from arbitrary subsets of available sensors, robustly handling missing modalities and producing physically plausible motions. Third, we conduct a systematic study of sensor complementarity, quantifying how different modalities complement one another. Code and dataset are available at https://vcai.mpi-inf.mpg.de/projects/WHIP/

</details>


### [10] [Compression Asymmetry and Trajectory Binding in Noise-Anchored Diffusion Inversion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.09784)
*Yongseong Park,Joeun Kim,HoEun Kim,Young-Sik Kim*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文研究了真实图像扩散反演中的质量-成本权衡，揭示了两个关键机制：元素级压缩不对称性和轨迹绑定/评分先验耦合。基于这些发现，提出了NARC方法，通过存储单个int8潜变量锚点并采用噪声水平依赖性权重调度，在PIE-Bench++上以比PnP DirectInv少约400倍的存储开销实现了+3.24 dB的PSNR提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 扩散反演面临质量与计算/存储成本的权衡问题，现有方法在效率与重建质量之间难以兼顾，本文旨在揭示有效存储噪声反演的核心机制并设计更优方案。

Method: 通过分析扩散轨迹中的前向高斯噪声锚点，识别出两种机制：1) 元素级压缩不对称性（int8全维锚点优于低维子空间摘要）；2) 轨迹绑定与评分先验耦合（需匹配前向锚点和训练好的评分网络）。基于此提出NARC，存储单个int8潜变量锚点，采用噪声水平依赖的锚点权重调度（强锚定于噪声主导阶段，宽松锚定于图像细节出现阶段）。

Result: 在PIE-Bench++上，NARC在Stable Diffusion 1.5中优于五种非精确基线方法，PSNR比PnP DirectInv高3.24 dB，存储开销减少约400倍。压缩不对称性、锚点特异性和编辑插件能力可迁移至SDXL 1024^2。

Conclusion: 扩散反演的最优存储策略是保留全维元素级锚点而非低维摘要，且反演过程依赖训练好的评分网络而非纯代数恒等。NARC方法实现了高效、高质量的反演，具有良好的可扩展性。

Abstract: Real-image diffusion inversion is governed by a tight quality-cost trade-off, with costs incurred in computation, storage, or per-image optimization. We study this trade-off through the forward Gaussian noise anchor that defines a diffusion trajectory and isolate two mechanisms behind effective stored-noise inversion. First, diffusion noise exhibits an element-wise compression asymmetry: int8 full-dimensional anchors preserve reconstruction, whereas low-dimensional subspace summaries are much less reliable, often collapsing even at comparable or smaller payloads; the element-wise over subspace ordering persists across five stored-noise inversion methods. Second, inversion is trajectory-bound and score-prior coupled: the matched forward anchor and a trained score network are both necessary, arguing against a purely algebraic-identity explanation. Together, these findings specify what to store and how to use it. They lead to Noise-Anchored Reverse Correction (NARC), a training-free inversion primitive that stores a single int8 latent anchor and reuses it with a fixed, noise-level-dependent anchor-weight schedule: strong anchoring when the reverse trajectory is noise-dominated, then relaxed anchoring as image detail emerges. On PIE-Bench++ with Stable Diffusion 1.5, NARC outperforms five modern non-exact baselines and improves PSNR by +3.24 dB over PnP DirectInv while using about 400x less inversion storage than PnP DirectInv. The compression asymmetry, anchor specificity, and editing plug-in also transfer to SDXL 1024^2.

</details>


### [11] [Lifelong Representations: A Survey on Continual Self-Supervised Learning for Vision Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.09785)
*Sergi Masip,Alicja Dobrzeniecka,Jonathan Swinnen,Joachim Collin,Bartłomiej Twardowski,Szymon Łukasik,Tinne Tuytelaars*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文系统综述了持续自监督学习（CSSL）在视觉领域的研究现状，分析了评估协议不一致性问题，解释了自监督目标对灾难性遗忘更具鲁棒性的原因，建立了统一的遗忘缓解策略分类体系，并指出了未来研究方向。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现实应用中（如机器人）模型需从无标签数据流中持续适应，而传统的持续学习假设有标签数据。CSSL领域发展迅速但缺乏系统性综述。

Method: 1) 分析现有评估协议并指出不一致问题；2) 从任务无关表征和更平滑的损失景观角度解释自监督目标对遗忘的鲁棒性；3) 构建统一的遗忘缓解策略分类体系（蒸馏、回放、正则化、架构方法、模型合并、目标级适应）。

Result: 揭示了CSSL评估标准不统一的问题，建立了系统性的方法分类体系，并指出需要从小型基准测试转向大规模持续预训练范式。

Conclusion: CSSL的推进需要超越小规模基准，发展面向大规模系统的持续预训练范式，同时解决可扩展性和快速适应性等开放挑战。

Abstract: Traditionally, continual learning has assumed access to labeled data, yet many real-world applications -- such as lifelong robotics -- require models to adapt continuously from unlabeled streams. This has led to the development of continual self-supervised learning (CSSL), a rapidly growing area that lacks a dedicated, systematic review. In this work, we present a comprehensive survey of CSSL for vision, with connections to emerging vision-language settings. First, we analyze existing evaluation protocols and highlight inconsistencies that hinder fair comparison. We then examine why self-supervised objectives exhibit improved robustness to catastrophic forgetting, relating this to task-agnostic representations and smoother loss landscapes. Next, we organize existing methods into a unified taxonomy based on their forgetting-mitigation strategies, including distillation, replay, regularization, architectural approaches, model merging, and objective-level adaptation. Finally, we identify open challenges such as scalability and the need for fast adaptability. We argue that advancing CSSL requires moving beyond small-scale benchmarks towards continual pre-training paradigms for large-scale systems.

</details>


### [12] [Adversarially Guided Diffusion for LiDAR Range Image Synthesis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.09787)
*Stavros Bouras,Antonios Makris,Alexandros Gkillas,Aris S. Lalos,Konstantinos Tserpes*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出首个基于扩散模型的非受限对抗攻击方法，针对2D距离图像语义分割任务，通过分割损失引导生成贴近LiDAR数据流形的对抗样本，实现可控且可迁移的分割错误。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有对抗攻击研究主要集中在图像分类和3D点云分割，但对2D距离图像（3D点云投影）的非受限对抗样本研究尚属空白，而这类攻击对自动驾驶安全性至关重要。

Method: 采用扩散模型，在采样过程中直接施加分割损失作为对抗引导，生成非受限对抗样本，使其保持在LiDAR数据流形附近同时诱导结构化分割错误。

Result: 在SemanticKITTI数据集上对RangeNet++和CENet进行测试，攻击提供可调节的退化程度，跨架构可迁移；与FGSM和SegPGD相比，在有效性与真实性之间取得更优平衡。

Conclusion: 扩散模型引导式对抗攻击能有效生成更真实、可控且可迁移的LiDAR语义分割对抗样本，为自动驾驶安全评估提供新工具。

Abstract: LiDAR semantic segmentation is a key perception task in autonomous driving, where false predictions can affect downstream planning and safety-critical decision-making. Although adversarial attacks, and specifically adversarial examples, have been widely studied for image classification and 3D point cloud segmentation, unrestricted adversarial examples remain largely unexplored in the space of 2D range images, which are projections of 3D point clouds. The proposed method is, to the best of our knowledge, the first diffusion-based unrestricted adversarial attack against 2D range-image segmentation, using adversarial guidance from a segmentation loss. By applying guidance directly during sampling, the method produces unrestricted adversarial examples that remain close to the learned LiDAR data manifold while inducing structured segmentation errors. Experiments on the SemanticKITTI dataset using RangeNet++ and CENet segmentation networks demonstrate that the attack provides adjustable degradation across guidance strengths and transfers across segmentation architectures. Compared with norm-bounded FGSM and SegPGD baselines, the proposed attack offers a distinct effectiveness-realism trade-off, achieving controllable white-box and transfer degradation while maintaining competitive distributional and visual realism.

</details>


### [13] [MVMGNN;Multi-View Masked Graph Neural Network for Alzheimer's Disease Diagnosis using Structural MRI](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.09788)
*Ni Yao,Zhenxu Wang,Danyang Sun,Chuang Han,Yanting Li,Jiaofen Nan,Fubao Zhu,Chen Zhao,Weihua Zhou*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于sMRI的多视图掩码图神经网络模型（MVMGNN），通过联合节点-边掩码机制和跨视图门控融合机制，用于阿尔茨海默病的早期诊断，在ADNI数据集上取得了优于现有方法的分类性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于sMRI的脑网络方法通常采用单一图构建策略，无法同时捕捉大脑区域之间的空间关系和形态相似性，限制了AD早期诊断的准确性。

Method: 提出MVMGNN模型，包括：(1) 联合节点-边掩码机制，同时选择影像组学特征维度和结构连接，减少图学习中的冗余；(2) 患者级跨视图门控融合机制，整合多视图表示。

Result: 在ADNI数据集上的实验表明，MVMGNN在AD分类任务中优于多种对比方法，可解释性分析显示模型能够识别与AD相关的关键脑区。

Conclusion: MVMGNN能够有效利用sMRI多视图信息进行AD早期诊断，并为脑网络中的判别模式提供有用见解，代码已开源。

Abstract: Alzheimer's disease (AD) is a common neurodegenerative disorder, and early diagnosis is of great significance for delaying disease progression and enabling timely intervention. Mild cognitive impairment (MCI), which represents an intermediate clinical stage between cognitively normal aging and AD. Structural magnetic resonance imaging (sMRI) provides detailed characterization of anatomical structures and plays an important role in AD-related brain analysis. However, existing sMRI-based brain network methods typically rely on a single graph construction strategy, limiting their ability to jointly capture spatial relationships and morphological similarities between brain regions. To address these issues, this paper proposes an sMRI-based multi-view masked graph neural network model (MVMGNN) for AD diagnosis. A joint node-edge masking mechanism is proposed to simultaneously select radiomics feature dimensions and structural connections, reducing redundancy during graph learning. Furthermore, a patient-level cross-view gated fusion mechanism is proposed to integrate multi-view representations. Experimental results on the ADNI dataset demonstrate that MVMGNN outperforms several competing approaches in AD classification. Interpretability analysis further demonstrates that MVMGNN is able to identify key brain regions associated with AD, providing useful insights into discriminative patterns in sMRI-based brain networks.Our implementation is publicly available at https://github.com/chenzhao2023/MVMGNN_AD

</details>


### [14] [Detangled: A Framework for Creating, Editing, and Inferencing Feature Rich Hair Strands](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.09811)
*Sarah Jobalia,Yitong Deng,Carolyn Smith,Ronald Fedkiw*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出了一个基于五维参数空间的头发丝纹理建模与生成框架，通过将发丝纹理与整体发型解耦，实现了从纹理参数到发丝生成的端到端控制，并利用生成式AI创建符合指定纹理的新发丝。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有头发建模方法未能有效区分发丝本身的纹理特征（卷曲、扭转等）与整体发型方向，缺乏一个既直观又精确的纹理描述体系。

Method: 1) 提出五维参数空间与自然发丝纹理一一对应；2) 通过中心线几何将发丝映射到规范纹理空间，实现纹理与发型解耦；3) 用新方法从复杂发型数据中提取干净的中心线；4) 训练两个神经网络：一个用于按五参数标注发丝，另一个作为生成式AI创建符合纹理参数的新发丝。

Result: 该方法支持纹理迁移和用户自定义输入的发型编辑，在多种发质上均展示了有效结果。

Conclusion: 通过解耦纹理与发型方向，结合参数化描述与生成式AI，为头发建模提供了更灵活、更可控的新途径。

Abstract: We present a framework for understanding and generating feature rich hair strands. Drawing upon both scientific and cultural expertise, we define strand texture as the various distinctive patterns (curling, switchbacks, twist, etc.) that are formed by forces internal to a hair strand. We begin by proposing a novel five-dimensional parameter space, intended to be a bijection with naturally occurring hair strand textures. This encoding is both qualitatively accessible, allowing users to readily locate their own hair in the parameter space, and quantitatively precise, allowing the generation of individual strands from texture inputs. Importantly, strand texture should be independent from the overall strand direction. In order to disentangle strand texture from the overall strand direction, we identify centerline geometry and use it to map strands into a canonical space (a strand texture space). We construct centerlines using a novel method that cleanly distills complex hair grooms, separating the strand texture from the overall style (parameterized by style guides). We enable the creation of new strands conforming to our parametric description of texture via a generative artificial intelligence approach supervised by a separate neural network trained to label candidate strands according to our five-parameter description. The ability to create new strands conforming to any desired texture enables groom editing using either texture transfer or user-provided inputs. We demonstrate results on a variety of hair types.

</details>


### [15] [Memory-Conditioned Tool Calling for Camera-First Visual Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.09822)
*Xiaofan Wu,Xi Zeng,Miaoxia Chen,Peishan Chen,Shuyan Li,Jiyun Yao,Hanyong Zhong,Jiahao Zhu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文研究了在仅接收图像的摄像头优先场景下，通过三层个人视觉记忆（档案、短期焦点、观察）来提升智能体工具选择和参数的用户对齐度。实验表明，移除完整记忆模块会使工具查询相关性下降0.47（11.2%），端到端效用下降0.082（9.7%）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在摄像头优先的设置中，用户只能发送图像，智能体需要自主决定查询内容。现有方法缺乏个人视觉记忆来指导工具选择和参数，导致结果不够用户对齐。作者希望通过引入个人视觉记忆来改善这一问题。

Method: 设计了三层个人视觉记忆结构：档案（profile）、短期焦点（short-term focus）、观察（observations）。每轮加载这些记忆来条件化LLM工具调用循环，并包含冲突感知的回写机制以更新用户模型。

Result: 在800张图像配合同步合成记忆块的消融实验中，移除三层记忆块导致工具查询相关性从4.21降至3.74（5分制，下降11.2%），端到端效用从0.842降至0.760（下降9.7%）。

Conclusion: 个人视觉记忆能有效提升智能体在摄像头优先场景下的工具选择和参数对齐度，但实验限于固定合成记忆块，尚未在真实用户历史的多会话回写场景中验证。

Abstract: Recognition tells an agent what is in an image; personal memory affects what is worth looking up next. In a camera-first setting the user can send only an image, so the agent must form the lookups. We study whether personal visual memory improves agent-side tool choice and tool arguments, and thereby more user-aligned multi-tool lookups. The design uses a three-layer personal visual memory (profile, short-term focus, observations) that is loaded on each turn to condition an LLM tool-calling loop under camera-first intake, and includes conflict-aware write-back intended to refresh the user model for later captures. On 800 images paired with synthetic memory blocks constructed for controlled ablation, removing the full three-layer memory block reduces tool-query relevance by 0.47 points absolute (4.21 -> 3.74 on a 5-point scale; 11.2% relative) and end-to-end utility by 0.082 absolute (0.842 -> 0.760; 9.7% relative). These results measure memory conditioning of tool policy under image-only intake with fixed synthetic blocks, not multi-session write-back from live user histories.

</details>


### [16] [Towards Objective Dysgraphia Detection: A Multi-Branch Deep Learning Approach for Online Handwriting Analysis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.09826)
*Lydia Ouhib,Yassine Ouzar,Zoé Pinseel,Stéphane Bouilland,Mehdi Ammi*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于深度学习的双分支框架，利用数字手写板采集的在线手写数据，融合手工特征、嵌入特征（MOMENT）、连续小波变换（CWT）和格拉米角场（GAF）图像表示，用于客观检测书写障碍，在DiaGraMo数据集上验证了融合方案的有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统书写障碍诊断依赖临床医生主观观察，耗时长且易产生 variability，缺乏客观、自动化的检测方法。

Method: 构建双分支深度学习框架：第一分支从原始时序信号提取手工和嵌入（MOMENT）运动学特征；第二分支利用CWT和GAF将时序信号转换为图像表示；最终融合两类特征进行检测。

Result: 在DiaGraMo数据集上，GAF、MOMENT与手工运动学特征的融合方案优于各单独表示及其他融合方案。

Conclusion: 图像与信号表示的互补性有助于实现更客观的书写障碍检测。

Abstract: Dysgraphia is a specific learning disability that is prevalent among school-age children. It affects handwriting coherence, quality, fluency, and legibility, often hindering academic achievement and early learning development. This motor coordination disorder is typically diagnosed through subjective assessments based on clinician observation, which can be timeconsuming and prone to variability. In this paper, we introduce a deep learning-based framework for objective dysgraphia detection using online handwriting data captured via digitizing tablets. The proposed framework relies on two complementary branches: the first pipeline extracts both handcrafted and embedding-based kinematic features directly from raw temporal signals, while the second leverages image-based representations of the temporal signals generated using continuous wavelet transforms (CWT) and Gramian Angular Fields (GAF). The resulting features are then fused to leverage the complementary strengths of both representations. The four representations were evaluated separately and jointly using the publicly available DiaGraMo dataset, showing that the fusion of GAF, MOMENT, and hand-crafted kinematic features outperforms each individual representation, as well as other fusion schemes. These findings highlight the potential of the complementarity of image and signal based representations for more objective dysgraphia detection.

</details>


### [17] [TDSal: Task-Based Top-Down Saliency Prediction Model](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.09827)
*Can Mizrakli,Tolga K. Capin*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于自然语言任务描述的条件化视觉显著性模型，能够预测不同任务意图下的注意力分布，从而更准确地模拟目标驱动的视觉注意。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有显著性模型大多针对自由观看条件设计，忽略了人类注意往往受到明确任务目标的影响，因此需要一种能够结合任务语义的显著性预测方法。

Method: 提出一个条件化视觉显著性模型，将自然语言任务描述作为输入，与图像特征结合，生成任务依赖的显著性图，反映不同观看意图下的注意力变化。

Result: 通过定量和定性分析表明，引入显式任务语义能够更准确地建模目标导向的视觉注意力。

Conclusion: 自然语言任务描述可以有效引导视觉显著性预测，使模型更贴近真实人类在任务驱动下的注意机制。

Abstract: Visual saliency aims to predict the regions of an image most likely to attract human visual attention. While most saliency models assume free-viewing conditions, human attention is often shaped by explicit task goals. In this work, we address task-driven saliency prediction by proposing a model that conditions visual attention on natural-language task descriptions. The model produces task-dependent saliency maps that reflect how attention shifts under different viewing intents. Through quantitative and qualitative analysis, we show that incorporating explicit task semantics enables more faithful modeling of goal-directed visual attention.

</details>


### [18] [Does YOLO26 Truly Offer Advantages Over Its Predecessors for Edge Deployment? A Benchmark Study in Aquaculture](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.09835)
*Rakesh Ranjan,Gajanan S. Kothawade,Kata Sharrer,Scott Tsukuda,Christopher Good*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本研究对YOLO26及其前代模型在鱼类死亡检测任务上进行了全面基准测试，发现所有模型在充足数据下性能相当（mAP50仅差1.04个百分点），但存在数据效率和部署性能的权衡：YOLOv8数据效率最高（400张图片达90% mAP50），而YOLO26n在树莓派5上推理最快（7.51 FPS）。结果表明模型选择需综合考虑数据、硬件和推理需求。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: YOLO26架构虽已提出并声称适合边缘水产养殖应用，但在水产养殖特定场景下的性能、运行效率和部署适用性尚未得到系统验证。

Method: 对YOLO26与YOLOv5u、YOLOv8、YOLO11三种前代模型在nano、small、medium三种尺度共12个变体进行基准测试，评估检测精度、七种数据集规模下的训练效率，以及在NVIDIA A100 GPU和树莓派5上的推理性能。

Result: 1) 充足数据下所有模型性能相近（mAP50仅差1.04个百分点）；2) YOLOv8数据效率最高（400张图片达90% mAP50），YOLO26 nano/small需1000张；3) YOLO26n在树莓派5上推理最快（7.51 FPS）；4) YOLOv5mu在CPU硬件上优于所有当代中型架构。

Conclusion: 架构新颖性本身不足以指导模型选择，实际部署时需联合考虑训练数据可用性、目标硬件和推理需求来选择水产养殖边缘AI目标检测模型。

Abstract: The recently introduced YOLO26 architecture incorporates NMS-free end-to-end inference and is optimized for deployment on resource-constrained CPU-based devices, making it well-suited for edge-based aquaculture applications. However, its performance, operational efficiency, and deployment suitability have not been systematically validated in aquaculture-specific scenarios. This study presents a comprehensive benchmark of YOLO26 against three Ultralytics predecessors (YOLOv5u, YOLOv8, and YOLO11) across nano, small, and medium model scales for fish mortality detection, a critical indicator of fish population health and welfare. Twelve model variants were evaluated for detection accuracy, training efficiency across seven dataset sizes, and inference performance on high-performance NVIDIA A100 GPUs and a CPU-only Raspberry Pi 5 edge platform. All models achieved comparable performance on the full dataset, with mAP50 differing by only 1.04 percentage points, indicating that architectural generation has little influence on final detection accuracy when sufficient training data are available. However, clear trade-offs emerged in data efficiency and deployment performance. YOLOv8 achieved 90% mAP50 with only 400 training images, whereas the YOLO26 nano and small variants required 1,000 images to reach comparable accuracy. Conversely, YOLO26n achieved the highest inference speed on the Raspberry Pi 5 (7.51 FPS), while YOLOv5mu outperformed all contemporary medium-scale architectures on CPU-based hardware. These results show that architectural novelty alone is insufficient for model selection and that training data availability, target hardware, and inference requirements should be considered jointly when selecting object detection models for practical edge AI deployment in aquaculture.

</details>


### [19] [Reliability-Aware Ensemble Classification Under Class Imbalance: A Calibration Study on Liquid-Based Cervical Cytology](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.09837)
*Nisreen Albzour,Sarah S. Lam*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文在Mendeley LBC数据集上研究类别不平衡感知和校准感知的集成分类方法，发现校准是提升模型可靠性的关键因素，而集成规模影响不大。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 宫颈细胞学分类模型通常基于平衡数据集评估，但真实LBC样本常存在小样本和类别不平衡问题，需要更贴近实际场景的研究。

Method: 使用三种轻量级架构（Swin-Tiny、TinyViT-5M、DenseNet121）结合加权随机采样，以及两种软投票集成（Hybrid-2、Hybrid-3），并在交叉验证中独立划分校准子集进行温度缩放后验校准。

Result: 校准显著降低了ECE、Brier分数和负对数似然，但判别指标基本不变；所有错误分类均局限于HSIL与SCC边界，未涉及NILM和LSIL；集成规模未带来额外可靠性提升。

Conclusion: 对于该数据集，校准是提升可靠性的主导因素，而非集成规模；但结论受限于数据集规模较小。

Abstract: Cervical cytology classification models are typically evaluated on curated, class-balanced benchmarks, but real-world liquid-based cytology (LBC) collections are often small and class-imbalanced. This paper presents a class-imbalance-aware and calibration-aware ensemble classification study on the Mendeley LBC dataset, using its native four-class Bethesda taxonomy (NILM, LSIL, HSIL, SCC) rather than a collapsed binary formulation. Three lightweight architectures (Swin-Tiny, TinyViT-5M, DenseNet121) are trained directly on Mendeley LBC using weighted random sampling to counteract class imbalance, and compared against two soft-voting ensembles (Hybrid-2, Hybrid-3). Post-hoc temperature scaling is fit on a held-out calibration subset carved out of the training portion of each cross-validation fold, distinct from both the training data used to fit model weights and the evaluation fold used for final metrics, avoiding the optimistic calibration estimates that result when the same data is used for both purposes. Calibration substantially reduces expected calibration error, Brier score, and negative log-likelihood for every model and ensemble configuration tested, while discrimination metrics (accuracy, macro-F1, macro-AUROC) remain essentially unchanged. Ensemble size shows no consistent additional reliability benefit over the best individual model once all configurations are properly calibrated. Confusion matrices show that all classification errors, across every configuration, are confined to the boundary between high-grade lesions (HSIL) and carcinoma (SCC); no errors involve the negative (NILM) or low-grade (LSIL) categories. These results suggest that, for this dataset, calibration is the dominant lever for reliability, not ensemble size, though this conclusion should be read in light of the dataset's modest size.

</details>


### [20] [Prompting-MammAlps: Fine-Grained Text-to-Video Retrieval for Camera-Trap Data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.09876)
*Valentin Gabeff,Baptiste Maquignaz,Jennifer Shan,Sepideh Mamooler,Gencer Sumbul,Blair Costelloe,Devis Tuia,Alexander Mathis*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出Prompting-MammAlps，首个相机陷阱视频检索基准，并设计了一种基于视觉Transformer和LLM的细粒度可解释检索方法，在生态视频检索任务上显著优于零样本VLM方法（F1: 34% vs 18%）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有文本到视频检索方法缺乏时空理解能力，且难以泛化到生态数据（如相机陷阱视频），亟需一个针对该领域的基准和可解释的检索方法。

Method: 1) 训练视觉Transformer进行时空动作定位，将输出转为结构化文本描述每个视频；2) 基于动物行为学启发的查询由LLM编码代理处理，利用自定义解析库函数进行结构化文本解析和视频检索，减少幻觉风险并增强可解释性。

Result: 在Prompting-MammAlps基准的135个生态相关查询和775个视频上，方法F1为34%，而最佳零样本VLM仅18%。

Conclusion: 该细粒度可解释方法在相机陷阱视频检索任务上优于现有VLM方法，且具有良好的可解释性，为生态监控数据分析提供了有效工具。

Abstract: Automatically retrieving videos from large camera-trap datasets remains challenging. Text-to-Video retrieval (TVR) methods based on large video-language models (VLMs) have potential to retrieve events of interest by describing them with simple text queries. However, current methods often lack spatiotemporal understanding and do not generalize well to ecological data. In this work, we introduce Prompting-MammAlps, the first camera-trap TVR benchmark, and propose a fine-grained and interpretable TVR method. Specifically, we trained a vision transformer to perform spatiotemporal action localization, and convert its output to structured text, describing each video. Independently, ethology-inspired queries are processed by a Large-Language Model (LLM) based coding agent to parse the structured text per video and retrieve videos accordingly. We harnessed the LLM to use functions from a custom parsing library to minimize the risk of LLM hallucinations and to improve method interpretability. This retrieval approach applied on the Prompting-MammAlps benchmark achieved a set-based F1-score of 34\% on a test set of 135 ecologically-relevant queries and 775 candidate videos. In comparison the best zero-shot VLM achieved a F1-score of 18\%, while also lacking interpretability. Project page: https://cnai.epfl.ch/prompting-mammalps

</details>


### [21] [A Dual-Stream Challenge-Response Protocol for Ocular Liveness Verification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.09883)
*Ismail Kably*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种时空-亮度传感器融合协议，通过双流挑战-响应框架结合平滑追踪和瞳孔光反射，构建同步矩阵模型来检测眼部呈现攻击，蒙特卡洛仿真验证了理论可行性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有眼部活体检测方法依赖单一生理指标（如注视追踪或瞳孔光反射），容易被高清视频回放和实时生成深度伪造独立欺骗，亟需联合多模态指标以提升抗攻击能力。

Method: 提出双流挑战-响应框架：生成随机时空变化的视觉刺激，同时引发平滑追踪眼动和瞳孔收缩反应；构建同步矩阵（Synchronization Matrix）作为状态空间似然模型，评估两种生理响应与刺激之间的连续交叉相关性。

Result: 基于文献推导的延迟分布进行蒙特卡洛仿真，证明了真实响应和模拟攻击之间的理论可分离性；多轮挑战设计在存在非零渲染延迟差距时可提升对生成深度伪造的检测能力。

Conclusion: 该工作为下一代眼部生物识别的动态欺骗防御提供了仿真支持的理论框架，但人类受试者验证是部署前必要的后续工作。

Abstract: Ocular biometric systems face sophisticated presentation attacks, including high-resolution video replays and real-time generative deepfakes, which easily bypass static liveness checks. Current Presentation Attack Detection (PAD) frameworks typically rely on isolated physiological metrics, such as gaze tracking or the Pupillary Light Reflex (PLR), which can be spoofed independently. This paper proposes a Spatio-Luminance Sensor Fusion protocol, which introduces a dual-stream challenge-response framework for ocular liveness verification by uniting these metrics into a simultaneous authentication challenge. By generating a randomized, time-varying visual stimulus that fluctuates in both spatial trajectory and luminance intensity, we construct a mathematically coupled state-space likelihood model, termed the Synchronization Matrix, to evaluate the continuous cross-correlation between the expected biological latencies of smooth pursuit tracking and pupillary constriction. Using Monte Carlo simulation grounded in literature-derived latency distributions, we demonstrate theoretical separability between genuine and simulated attack conditions, and show that a multi-round challenge design improves the detection of generative deepfakes when a non-zero rendering-latency gap exists. This work provides a simulation-supported theoretical framework for next-generation dynamic spoofing defense in ocular and iris biometrics; human-subject validation is identified as necessary future work before deployment claims can be made.

</details>


### [22] [ShapKO: Shapley-Adaptive Modality Knockout for Robust Multimodal Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.09884)
*Nusrat Binta Nizam,Fengbei Liu,Sunwoo Kwak,Minh Nguyen,Ruining Deng,Mert R. Sabuncu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出ShapKO动态训练策略，通过Shapley值自适应调整模态遮罩概率，解决多模态医学模型在模态缺失和模态主导问题下的性能退化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多模态医学模型在现实中常面临输入模态缺失的问题，且训练过程中存在模态主导现象（过度依赖预测性强的模态而忽略互补模态），导致部分模态可用时鲁棒性差。现有训练时模态剔除方法使用静态遮罩率，无法适应训练过程中模态效用的变化。

Method: ShapKO动态训练策略：周期性评估模态子集上的验证性能，通过Shapley值估计模态重要性，据此动态更新各模态的遮罩概率，更频繁地抑制主导模态，促进互补表示学习，无需修改网络架构。

Result: 在多任务临床分类、生存预测和癌症检测三个数据集上，ShapKO在模态缺失情况下持续提升性能，并产生可解释的遮罩学习行为轨迹。

Conclusion: ShapKO通过自适应模态剔除有效提升了多模态模型在部分模态缺失时的鲁棒性，且具有可解释性。

Abstract: Multimodal medical models often degrade when inputs are missing, a common scenario in real-world clinical workflows. Separately, even when all modalities are present, modality dominance is observed during training, where optimization over-relies on a highly predictive modality and undertrains complementary sources, resulting in poor robustness under partial availability. While training-time modality knockout improves missing-modality robustness, existing approaches use static masking rates that cannot adapt to evolving modality utility during training. We introduce ShapKO (Shapley-Adaptive Modality Knockout), a dynamic training strategy that learns modality-specific knockout probabilities based on validation utility. ShapKO periodically evaluates performance across modality subsets, estimates modality importance via Shapley values, and updates masking probabilities to suppress dominant modalities more frequently. This adaptive process promotes complementary representations, while requiring no architectural modifications. We evaluate ShapKO on three datasets covering multitask clinical classification, survival prediction, and cancer detection. ShapKO consistently improves performance under modality absence and yields interpretable trajectories of learned masking behavior. Code is available at: https://github.com/sumona00/ShapKO

</details>


### [23] [Bridging the Catalog-to-Real Gap: Scalable Product Recognition via Multi-Stage Contrastive Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.09888)
*Anyi Zhang,Joy Mazumder,Kiril Lomakin*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出Cat2Real框架，通过多阶段对比学习将产品识别重构为跨域检索问题，实现从目录图像到真实场景的零样本泛化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大规模应用中，将真实场景商品图像与庞大企业目录进行准确匹配存在严重的可扩展性瓶颈，传统闭集分类方法难以应对真实图像与目录图像之间的巨大域差异。

Method: 将产品识别重构为基于嵌入的跨域检索问题，提出Cat2Real（Catalog-to-Real）多阶段对比学习范式，利用物品级和图像级相似性进行针对性难负样本挖掘，微调视觉骨干网络以弥合域差距。

Result: 该方法可无缝扩展到未见过的产品和类别，在完全没有真实训练图像的情况下仍展现出优异的零样本泛化性能。

Conclusion: 通过跨域对比学习范式有效解决了大规模产品识别中的域差距和可扩展性问题，实现了对新增库存的零样本识别能力。

Abstract: Automated product recognition is a cornerstone of modern retail intelligence; however, accurately matching real-world, in-store images against extensive corporate catalogs remains a major scalability bottleneck for large-scale applications. In this work, we address this challenge by reformulating the task as an embedding-based cross-domain retrieval problem rather than a standard closed-set classification task. Specifically, we define the objective as retrieving the most corresponding catalog reference image for a given real-world product query crop from an expansive inventory. To bridge the severe domain gap between pristine studio packshots and noisy in-store queries, we introduce a novel catalog-to-real multi-stage contrastive learning paradigm (Cat2Real). This framework fine-tunes a vision backbone by systematically exploiting both item-level and image-level similarities to drive targeted hard negative mining. Extensive empirical evaluations demonstrate that our paradigm scales seamlessly to unseen products and categories, yielding outstanding zero-shot generalization performance even in the complete absence of real-world training images for novel inventory.

</details>


### [24] [Do Transformer Temporal Heads and Post-Pooling Motion Gates Help CorrNet-based CSLR? An Empirical Study](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.09890)
*Lisi Wang,Zhidong Xiao,Jianjun Peng*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文对连续手语识别（CSLR）中CorrNet基线系统进行两项自然扩展研究：用Transformer替换BiLSTM时序头，以及在时序池化后注入运动线索。结果表明Transformer并未优于BiLSTM，而运动注入模块MotionGate退化恒等映射，说明这些看似自然的扩展在实际中并不有效，需谨慎验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索CorrNet基线系统的两种自然扩展（Transformer时序头和运动线索注入）是否能进一步提升连续手语识别（CSLR）性能。

Method: 1) 将CorrNet的BiLSTM时序头替换为Transformer编码器，并调整训练策略。2) 设计轻量级模块MotionGate，在时序池化后注入运动线索。

Result: 1) Transformer头未优于BiLSTM基线，两者计算量和运行时成本几乎相同。2) MotionGate退化恒等映射：门控失去运动选择性，注入的残差变为弱非选择性扰动。

Conclusion: CorrNet的相关性编码后显式注入运动信息基本冗余；CSLR中的自然架构扩展需谨慎测试，不能假设其对性能有帮助。

Abstract: CorrNet is a strong baseline for continuous sign language recognition (CSLR) because it models inter-frame correlations inside the visual encoding stage. In this paper, we study two natural extensions of a reproduced CorrNet system: replacing the BiLSTM temporal head with a Transformer encoder, and injecting motion cues after temporal pooling. We find that the Transformer head does not outperform the BiLSTM baseline, even with a training strategy adjusted for the Transformer, and the two heads have almost the same computational and runtime cost. For the second extension, we design a lightweight module called MotionGate. In our experiments, MotionGate consistently collapses to an identity-like mapping: the gate loses motion selectivity, and the injected residual becomes a weak, non-selective perturbation of the pooled features. These results suggest that explicit motion injection after CorrNet's correlation-based encoding is largely redundant, and that natural-looking architectural extensions in CSLR should be tested carefully instead of being assumed to help.

</details>


### [25] [Banshee: Target Switch Attacks on Gimbal-Stabilized Visual Tracking Systems via Acoustic Injection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.09930)
*Jiarui Li,Joseph Brewington,Qingzhao Zhang,Z. Morley Mao*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: Banshee是一种通过声学信号干扰无人机云台相机系统，从而诱导视觉跟踪目标切换的物理可实现的攻击方法，在仿真和实物实验中均取得了超过93%的攻击成功率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有研究表明声学信号可以干扰云台内部，但此类攻击在无人机跟踪和跟随等实际应用中的影响尚未被充分探索，且现有演示忽略了物体运动不确定性和运行时延迟等实际挑战。

Method: 提出Banshee攻击框架，通过生成精心设计的声学波形，诱导云台产生优化的对抗性振荡，导致相机视角出现方向性漂移，从而破坏帧间目标关联，迫使跟踪器从原始目标切换到攻击者选择的物体。

Result: 在仿真中针对两个商用云台系统和五个跟踪器达到93.6%成功率；在真实场景中对商用无人机的黑盒攻击达到95.5%成功率。

Conclusion: 揭示了一种声学与视觉之间的跨域安全漏洞，强调了云台系统及应用需要鲁棒性设计。

Abstract: Gimbal-stabilized visual tracking is critical for modern autonomous systems such as Unmanned Aerial Vehicles (UAVs). While prior work shows acoustic signals can disturb gimbal internals, the impact of such attacks on real-world applications like UAV tracking and following remains underexplored. Existing demonstrations largely overlook practical challenges for real-world attacks, such as object-motion uncertainty and runtime latency. To bridge this gap, we present Banshee, the first physically realizable attack that induces target switching in UAV visual tracking systems by exploiting acoustic vulnerabilities in gimbal-camera systems. Banshee generates carefully crafted acoustic waveforms that induce optimized adversarial gimbal oscillations, causing directionally biased camera-view drifts that break inter-frame target associations. Consequently, the onboard tracker is driven to switch from the original target to an attacker-selected object with high probability, with occasional target loss. Banshee achieves a 93.6% success rate in simulation across two commercial gimbal systems and five trackers. Real-world benchtop and in-flight black-box attacks against a commercial drone across varied scenarios show an overall 95.5% attack success rate. Our results reveal a practical cross-domain vulnerability between acoustics and vision, highlighting the need for robust designs of gimbal systems and applications. Our code is available at: https://github.com/U1ltra/Banshee.

</details>


### [26] [UniPose9D: Universal Category-Agnostic Object Pose Estimation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.09985)
*Yang You,Yi Du,Cole Harrison,Leonidas Guibas*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: UniPose9D是一种类别无关的基础模型，无需类别标签、CAD模型或先验形状，仅通过RGB-D或RGB+预测深度即可实现9D物体姿态估计，在六个数据集上匹配或超越专门方法，并能泛化到未见物体和野外场景。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有物体姿态估计方法虽然性能强劲，但往往过拟合于现有基准，对新颖类别和未见场景的泛化能力有限。作者希望提出一种类别无关的基础模型，摆脱对类别标签、CAD模型、平均形状先验等约束。

Method: 从观测几何中采样点对，利用DINOv2和PointNet特征预测NOCS坐标；提出基于点对的RANSAC N跳Kabsch-Umeyama算法（自适应阈值）提高精度；采用流匹配（flow matching）处理对称歧义；通过整理和对齐现有公开数据集构建大规模训练集。

Result: 在六个数据集上的实验表明，单一统一模型能够匹配或超越专门方法，并且能够泛化到未见物体和野外场景。

Conclusion: UniPose9D作为类别无关的基础模型，在物体姿态估计任务上展现了强泛化能力，代码和模型已开源。

Abstract: Object pose estimation is a fundamental problem in 3D vision. Although recent state-of-the-art approaches achieve strong performance, they often overfit to existing benchmarks and exhibit limited generalization to novel categories and unseen scenes. We propose UniPose9D, a category-agnostic foundation model for 9D object pose estimation: given an instance mask/ROI and either an RGB-D observation or an RGB image with predicted depth, the model estimates rotation, translation, and metric size without category labels, CAD models, mean-shape priors, or reference views. Specifically, UniPose9D samples point pairs from the observed object geometry and uses DINOv2 and PointNet features to predict NOCS coordinates for each pair. To improve accuracy, we introduce a point-pair-based RANSAC N-hop Kabsch--Umeyama algorithm with an adaptive threshold. We further employ flow matching to address symmetric ambiguities and construct a large-scale training set by curating and aligning pose annotations from existing public datasets. Experiments across six datasets show that a single unified model can match or surpass specialist methods while generalizing to unseen objects and in-the-wild scenarios. Our code and model are available on https://github.com/qq456cvb/UniPose9D.

</details>


### [27] [Model Guides You How to Draw: Adaptive Visual Gating for Unified Multimodal Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.10004)
*Wenxi Gao,Guanxi Lu,Didi Zhu,Hao Mark Chen,Quan Deng,Zhican Wang,Jiankang Deng,Hongxiang Fan*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出AdaViG方法，通过内部信号（生成意图和视觉保真度）动态判断是否生成中间视觉推理步骤，避免误导性视觉证据并降低计算开销，在视觉数学推理任务上提升准确率最高5.7%，同时减少25%-91%的视觉生成FLOPs和15.4%-45.6%的延迟。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 统一多模态模型在视觉数学推理中生成的中间视觉步骤不一定有益，甚至可能因引入错误视觉证据误导后续推理，且频繁触发视觉步骤导致大量计算和内存开销，影响推理效率。

Method: 提出AdaViG，一种无需训练的适应性视觉门控方法。利用模型内部两个信号——生成意图（模型是否有具体的文本绘制计划）和视觉保真度（视觉生成是否基于原始输入图像），在视觉步骤生成的早期阶段动态评估，当两个信号均较弱时中止视觉生成，防止误导性视觉证据进入推理链并避免不必要计算。

Result: 准确率提升最高5.7%，视觉生成FLOPs减少25.0%-91.0%，端到端延迟降低15.4%-45.6%。

Conclusion: 通过动态门控视觉生成步骤，既能提升推理准确性又能大幅提高效率，验证了利用模型内部信号判断视觉步骤有效性的可行性。

Abstract: Unified multimodal models (UMMs) with interleaved reasoning, which generate both textual and visual steps as part of intermediate reasoning traces, have demonstrated great potential for visual mathematical reasoning tasks. However, we identify a key insight in this paradigm: generating intermediate visual reasoning steps is not always beneficial and can even be harmful, as self-generated visual steps may introduce erroneous visual evidence that misleads subsequent reasoning. Moreover, frequently triggering visual steps during reasoning incurs substantial computational and memory overhead, degrading inference efficiency. To address these accuracy and efficiency challenges, we observe that the model's internal signals can indicate whether a visual step will benefit reasoning before the entire visual generation is completed. Specifically, this work identifies two internal signals: 1) Generation Intent, which reflects whether the model has a concrete textual plan for what to draw, and 2) Visual Fidelity, which measures whether the visual generation remains grounded in the original input image. Leveraging these internal signals, we propose AdaViG, a training-free adaptive visual gating method for unified multimodal reasoning. AdaViG dynamically evaluates each triggered visual step at an early visual generation stage and aborts it when both signals are weak, thereby preventing misleading visual evidence from entering the reasoning trace while avoiding unnecessary computation. Comprehensive experiments demonstrate that AdaViG improves accuracy by up to 5.7% while reducing visual generation FLOPs by 25.0%-91.0% and wall-clock latency by 15.4%-45.6%.

</details>


### [28] [FlashBEV: Fast and Memory-Efficient Exact BEV Transformation with IO-Awareness](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.10071)
*Shunsuke Yokokawa,Hironori Kasahara*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: FlashBEV通过重新设计采样型视图变换的执行策略，消除了对高度相关中间张量的显式实例化，使峰值GPU内存降低一个数量级以上，且内存与高度分箱数无关。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 采样型视图变换（Sampling-VT）是BEV感知的关键组件，但其标准张量化实现在部署时会显式实例化巨大的高度相关中间张量，导致内存和延迟成本随垂直分辨率和相机数量急剧增长，成为部署瓶颈。

Method: 从算子执行角度分析，发现Tensorized Sampling-VT遵循gather-reduction模式——每个BEV查询独立跨相机和高度箱累积贡献。基于此提出FlashBEV，采用全融合且IO感知的执行策略，通过线程本地累积与即时重计算，避免实例化高度和相机相关的中间张量。

Result: 峰值GPU内存降低一个数量级以上，推理延迟显著加速，内存与高度分箱数无关，降至O(BCXY)（仅输出）。在固定部署预算下支持更高的BEV范围/分辨率和垂直离散化。

Conclusion: FlashBEV通过执行策略重设计（相同数学运算，不同执行方式）消除了Sampling-VT在部署中的关键可扩展性障碍，使其更适合内存受限设备上的部署。

Abstract: Bird's-eye-view (BEV) perception is a core component of camera-based 3D understanding in autonomous driving, where view transformation (VT) maps multi-camera image features into a unified BEV representation. Sampling-based view transformation (Sampling-VT) is attractive because it supports dense and continuous BEV aggregation for high-resolution and long-range perception. Its deployment bottleneck, however, is systems-level: standard tensorized implementations of Sampling-VT -- which we refer to as Tensorized Sampling-VT -- explicitly materialize large height-dependent intermediate tensors, causing memory and latency costs that scale poorly with vertical resolution and the number of cameras. We revisit Tensorized Sampling-VT from an operator-execution perspective and show that it follows a gather-reduction pattern: each BEV query independently accumulates contributions across cameras and height bins, enabling thread-local accumulation with on-the-fly recomputation that eliminates the need to materialize height- and camera-dependent intermediates. Based on this insight, we propose FlashBEV, a fully fused and IO-aware execution strategy mathematically equivalent to Tensorized Sampling-VT (same operator output) while substantially reducing global memory traffic and kernel-launch overhead. Experiments show that FlashBEV achieves more than an order of magnitude lower peak GPU memory and significant inference-latency speedups, with memory effectively independent of the number of height bins, reducing the operator's peak memory to O(BCXY) (output only). This unlocks higher BEV range/resolution and vertical discretization within fixed deployment budgets on memory-constrained devices. Our contribution is an execution redesign -- same math, different execution -- that removes a key scalability barrier for deployment-ready Sampling-VT. Code available at https://github.com/yokosyun/FlashBEV

</details>


### [29] [Label-Free Target-Domain Adaptation for Unconstrained Event-Image Feature Matching via Dual-Stage Distillation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.10082)
*Zhonghua Yi,Hao Shi,Qi Jiang,Yufan Zhang,Kailun Yang,Kaiwei Wang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出了一种无需标注和标定硬件的新型跨模态训练范式，通过标签无关的蒸馏预训练和外极线引导的自蒸馏框架，实现事件与图像数据间的像素级匹配，在MVSEC和TUM-VIE位姿估计任务上达到SOTA。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有跨模态匹配方法依赖匹配标签或严格对齐的硬件，无法适用于无标注、无约束的真实场景。

Method: 两阶段训练范式：1）大规模数据上的标签无关蒸馏预训练（分布损失+对比损失）；2）外极线引导的自蒸馏框架，利用一致性验证和几何置信度实现无监督自我进化。

Result: 在MVSEC和TUM-VIE位姿估计任务上达到SOTA性能。

Conclusion: 该方法有效解决了无标注、无约束场景下的跨模态匹配问题，并发布了新的评估基准。

Abstract: Building pixel-level correspondence between event and image data is a fundamental task for multi-sensor systems. However, existing cross-modal matching methods are largely restricted by their reliance on either matching labels or strictly aligned hardware, which limits them to unlabeled and unconstrained real-world scenarios where neither matching ground truth nor prior sensor relationships are available. To address this, we propose a novel two-stage training paradigm. First, we leverage large-scale data to perform label-agnostic distillation pretraining, upgrading optimization objectives with distribution-based and contrastive losses to learn highly generalizable representations. Second, to tackle unlabeled and unconstrained downstream data, we introduce an epipolar-guided self-distillation framework. By utilizing consistency verification to isolate robust matches and incorporating geometric confidence derived from an external epipolar prior, our model can effectively self-evolve directly on target domains without any supervision. Furthermore, we introduce a rigorous cross-modal evaluation benchmark based on TUM-VIE, featuring physically separated cameras with distinct intrinsic parameters and resolutions. Extensive experiments demonstrate that our proposed method achieves state-of-the-art performance on both MVSEC and TUM-VIE pose estimation tasks. The source code and benchmark will be made publicly available at https://github.com/ZhonghuaYi/nexus2-official.

</details>


### [30] [CVKD-UDA: Cross-View Knowledge Distillation for 3D Unsupervised Domain Adaptive Segmentation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.10087)
*Zhimin Yuan,Ming Cheng,Shangshu Yu,Wen Li,Dunqiang Liu,Xin Huang,Cheng Wang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出CVKD-UDA方法，通过调整体素大小构建领域相似表示，并利用跨视图知识蒸馏来改进3D无监督域自适应分割的预训练模型，有效平衡了模型的迁移性和判别性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有3D UDA方法依赖源域监督或输出级对抗对齐来获得预训练模型，但跨域差异大导致泛化能力有限且训练不稳定。构建领域相似表示是弥合这一差距的有效途径。

Method: 1) 通过改变体素大小生成两种互补视图，引入跨视图知识蒸馏(CVKD)增强模型泛化能力和目标域感知；2) 设计轻量级Decouple-Adapter和辅助模仿分类器，解耦跨视图知识转移，平衡迁移性和判别性。

Result: 在两个基准数据集上的大量实验表明，CVKD-UDA有效提升了自训练方法的性能。

Conclusion: CVKD-UDA为3D UDA分割提供了新的视角，代码将开源。

Abstract: 3D unsupervised domain adaptive (UDA) segmentation mitigates the high cost of manual annotations of the new domain data. Self-training has emerged as the dominant approach in this area, where its success heavily depends on a well-initialized warm-up model to generate reliable pseudo labels. However, existing methods often depend on source supervision or output-level adversarial alignment to obtain the warm-up model, which suffer from limited generalization and training instability due to the large domain gap between domains. Constructing domain-similar representations is an effective way to bridge this gap. In this work, we propose CVKD-UDA, which revisits voxel size as a core design factor to construct domain-similar representations and leverages cross-view complementary cues to balance transferability and discriminability of the warm-up model. First, we generate two complementary views by varying voxel sizes and introduce a cross-view knowledge distillation (CVKD) to enhance generalization and target perception of the model. Second, to balance transferability and discriminability, we design a lightweight Decouple-Adapter and an auxiliary imitation classifier to decouple cross-view knowledge transfer. Extensive experiments on two benchmarks demonstrate that CVKD-UDA effectively improves the performance of self-training methods and provides a new perspective for 3D UDA segmentation. Our code will be available at GitHub.

</details>


### [31] [EMBRACE: A Multi-task Framework for Comprehensive Quality Assessment in Cleavage-stage Embryo](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.10093)
*Anwar Hussain Sofi,Jung-Hua Wang,Ming-Jer Chen,Tsung-Hsien Lee,Yu-Chiao Yi,Ming-Kuan Lin,Yi-Chung Lai*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出了 EMBRACE，一个多任务深度学习框架，能够在 IVF 卵裂期胚胎显微镜图像上同时进行细胞质碎片分割、发育阶段分类（t2/t4）和卵裂球对称性分级，在测试集上取得了优异性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统卵裂期胚胎视觉评估存在观察者间变异，尤其是当碎片区域小、不规则或对比度低时，需要一种集成、自动化的评估方法。

Method: 采用共享 ResNet-50 骨干网络、C-MSFF 多尺度特征融合模块、U-Net 风格分割解码器和两个任务特定分类头，构建多任务学习框架。

Result: 碎片分割 Dice 0.781/IOU 0.677；发育阶段分类准确率 0.995，宏 F1 0.994，AUC 1.000；对称性分级平衡准确率 0.901，宏 F1 0.907，二次加权 Kappa 0.859。

Conclusion: 将空间可检测的碎片定位与胚胎整体形态评估结合在单一框架中是可行的，但临床应用前需进行外部和前瞻性验证。

Abstract: Cleavage-stage embryo assessment in in vitro fertilization requires the integrated interpretation of cytoplasmic fragmentation, developmental stage, and blastomere symmetry. However, conventional visual assessment is affected by observer variability, particularly when fragmented regions are small, irregular, or low contrast. This study presents EMBRACE, a multi-task deep learning framework for jointly performing cytoplasmic-fragmentation segmentation, t2/t4 developmental-stage classification, and blastomere-symmetry grading from static cleavage-stage embryo microscopy images. EMBRACE combines a shared ResNet-50 backbone, a concatenation-based multi-scale feature-fusion (C-MSFF) module, a U-Net-style segmentation decoder, and two task-specific classification heads. After predefined inclusion and exclusion criteria, 9,137 annotated embryo images were divided into 7,309 training, 914 validation, and 914 held-out test images. On the held-out test set, EMBRACE achieved a Dice coefficient of 0.781 and an intersection over union of 0.677 for fragmentation segmentation. Developmental-stage classification achieved an accuracy of 0.995, macro-F1 of 0.994, and AUC of 1.000. Blastomere-symmetry grading achieved a balanced accuracy of 0.901, macro-F1 of 0.907, and quadratic weighted kappa of 0.859. These findings support the feasibility of combining spatially inspectable fragmentation localization with embryo-level morphology assessment in a single framework. External and prospective validation is required before clinical deployment.

</details>


### [32] [LFD: Enabling Real-World Lensless Face Recognition with a Large-Scale Dataset](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.10094)
*Junho Kim,Salman S. Khan,Sara Wan,Tomi Kuye,Ashok Veeraraghavan*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文介绍了Lensless Face Dataset (LFD)，一个包含21,080张无镜头相机拍摄的人脸数据集，涵盖不同光照、角度和距离下的原始测量、重建图像和标准图像，旨在推动无镜头人脸识别研究。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统人脸识别系统依赖传统相机，存在体积大、成本高、隐私保护有限等局限。无镜头相机虽能解决这些问题，但其重建图像常含伪影，现有方法难以泛化到真实场景，且缺乏专门的数据集。

Method: 构建了包含21,080张人脸样本的LFD数据集，包含三种不同无镜头相机的原始测量数据、重建图像和标准图像，覆盖室内外多种光照、角度和距离条件，其中4,976张为户外自然光下采集。

Result: 通过全面评估和分析，证明LFD有效捕捉了不同无镜头成像设备间的共享特征和伪影，展示了跨不同无镜头相机的泛化能力。

Conclusion: LFD是推动无镜头人脸识别研究的重要数据集，有助于解决无镜头相机在真实场景中的泛化问题。

Abstract: Face recognition is a ubiquitously used computer vision task that has a wide range of applications ranging from everyday smartphone biometrics to high-stakes security systems. Most face recognition systems rely on traditional cameras, which often suffer from limitations such as bulky form factors, high costs, and limited privacy protection. To address these limitations, lensless cameras have emerged as an alternative. Lensless cameras use thin optical encoders, enabling smaller size, lower cost, and greater design flexibility. These cameras are typically paired with reconstruction algorithms that convert raw captures into recognizable images. However, reconstructed images often contain artifacts, and the reconstruction methods struggle to generalize well to real-world conditions. Furthermore, existing face datasets do not account for the artifacts present in lensless images. To address this issue, we introduce the Lensless Face Dataset (LFD). LFD comprises 21,080 lensless raw measurements, reconstructions, and standard images of faces captured under diverse lighting, angle, and distance. Our key contributions are: (1) Real-world lensless face data: LFD focuses on capturing a diverse face dataset with varying levels of artifacts introduced under different environments; (2) In-the-wild captures: 4,976 images are captured in outdoor settings with varying intensities of natural light and different background patterns; (3) Multiple lensless devices: LFD includes face images collected from three different types of lensless cameras, each with a unique optical encoder. We use this hardware diversity to demonstrate generalization across different lensless cameras. Through comprehensive evaluations and analysis, we show that LFD effectively captures shared features and artifacts across different lensless imaging devices, making it a valuable dataset for advancing lensless face recognition.

</details>


### [33] [DynaFilter: Cloud-driven Dynamic Filtering for Satellite Edge Intelligence](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.10098)
*Ziyang Zhang,Jie Liu,Luca Mottola*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: DynaFilter是一种面向卫星边缘系统的动态过滤技术，能在不解码完整数据的情况下，直接在压缩域中识别感兴趣区域（RoI），仅传输相关数据，显著降低带宽消耗和能耗。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有边缘-云系统要么需要先完全解压再分析，要么传输所有压缩数据，造成带宽浪费。卫星边缘系统带宽有限且连接不稳定，需要更高效的传输方案。

Method: 利用JPEG图像中的DC系数/AC能量和视频流中的运动向量等低级压缩语法与高级语义查询之间的强相关性，建立压缩域特征与云端查询语义的映射，实现选择性RoI推断。

Result: 图像像素数据解码量减少1.6x-7.1x；视频流带宽节省92.0%；设备能耗降低43.1-88.6%；推理延迟加速1.6x-3.0x。

Conclusion: DynaFilter在压缩域中实现高效的选择性推理，有效解决了卫星边缘系统带宽受限和能耗约束问题，性能优于现有基线方法。

Abstract: Modern satellite edge systems, including those performing remote sensing tasks such object detection and tracking, are characterized by severely limited bandwidth and intermittent connections, making continuous data transmission to the cloud impractical. Existing edge-cloud systems, however, either require heavy pre-processing before analysis, for instance, full decompression of imagery data, or transmit all compressed data regardless of relevance. To address these challenges, we design DynaFilter, a dynamic filtering technique that enables satellite edge devices to perform selective region-of-interest (RoI) inference directly in the compressed-domain, without full decompression. Our key insight is that low-level compression syntax, specifically DC coefficients/AC energy in JPEG images and motion vectors in video streams, exhibits strong correlations with high-level semantic queries. By establishing a precise mapping between cloud query semantics and multimodal compressed-domain features, DynaFilter enables the edge to identify and transmit only relevant data associated to RoIs. Extensive evaluations show that DynaFilter reduces the total volume of pixel data for decoding and subsequent inference by 1.6x-7.1x for images, and achieves 92.0% bandwidth savings for video streams compared to state-of-the-art baselines. Furthermore, it decreases energy consumption by 43.1-88.6% on target devices and achieves a 1.6x-3.0x speedup in inference latency.

</details>


### [34] [WeaveEarth: Structured Evidence Construction and Reasoning for Training-Free UHR Remote Sensing Understanding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.10120)
*Xianzhi Ma,Shujun Wang,Xiaohan Li,Hao Liu,Changhua Pei,Jianhui li*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: WeaveEarth是一个无需训练的高分辨率遥感图像理解框架，通过结构化证据构建与推理，在有限计算预算下提升视觉语言模型的全局-局部联合推理能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有UHR遥感图像理解方法（被动感知与主动感知）存在细粒度细节丢失、高延迟、上下文碎片化及错误累积等问题，亟需一种更高效的路径。

Method: 提出WeaveEarth框架，包含两个核心模块：1）全局感知证据构建，选择紧凑、低冗余、空间互补的最小支持证据集；2）结构化证据推理，将局部证据、空间元数据与相对拓扑织入统一推理接口，实现全局-局部联合推理。

Result: 在多个UHR遥感基准和多种冻结VLM骨干上，WeaveEarth一致优于强基线方法和现有UHR方法。

Conclusion: UHR遥感图像理解的有效路径不在于获取更多信息，而在于更优的组织方式，WeaveEarth验证了这一思路的有效性。

Abstract: Ultra-High-Resolution (UHR) remote sensing image understanding requires Vision-Language Models (VLMs) to capture both the global scene layout and sparse yet task-critical local details under limited computational budgets. Existing methods mainly follow two paradigms. One is passive perception, which relies on resolution expansion or token compression and may therefore discard fine-grained details. The other is active perception, which depends on multi-round zooming and search, but suffers from high latency, contextual fragmentation, and error accumulation. We argue that a more effective path toward UHR understanding lies not in accessing more, but in organizing better. To this end, we propose WeaveEarth, a training-free framework that reformulates UHR understanding as a problem of structured evidence construction and reasoning under global context constraints. Specifically, WeaveEarth first employs Global-Aware Evidence Construction to select a compact, low-redundancy, and spatially complementary Minimal Support Evidence Set. It then introduces Structured Evidence Reasoning, which weaves local evidence, spatial metadata, and relative topology into a unified reasoning interface, thereby enhancing the VLM's ability to perform global-local joint reasoning. Extensive experiments show that WeaveEarth consistently outperforms strong baselines and existing UHR methods across multiple UHR remote sensing benchmarks and multiple frozen VLM backbones. Code is available at https://github.com/XianZhi-Ma/WeaveEarth.

</details>


### [35] [TextGaze: Prompting Gaze Target Estimation with Textual Scene Cues](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.10130)
*Junhui She,Fei Wang,Kun Li,Yiqi Nie,Yuxin Liu,Zhangling Duan,Xun Yang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出TextGaze，一种利用大型视觉-语言模型（LVLM）作为可扩展语义指导的统一跨模态架构，用于估计场景中的人物注视目标位置，无需额外监督即可实现有竞争力的性能和跨数据集泛化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有注视目标估计方法存在两难：多分支方法需要额外监督和标注，导致标注负担高且阻碍领域迁移；而简化设计则优先考虑低级视觉显著性而忽略真实注视意图，导致预测注意力与实际注视目标不一致。

Method: TextGaze采用统一跨模态架构，从冻结编码器提取视觉特征，利用LVLM获取与注视对齐的文本线索，设计基于Transformer的融合模块并引入分层文本监督以保持任务语义，使用轻量化解码头联合预测注视热图和帧内/帧外状态。

Result: 在四个主流数据集上评估，TextGaze在关键指标上表现出有竞争力的性能，并展示了无需额外微调的鲁棒跨数据集泛化能力。

Conclusion: 提供了一种简化的替代设计方案，凸显了LVLM作为可访问辅助指导在注视估计任务中的潜力。

Abstract: Gaze target estimation aims to infer the position of a person's gaze within a scene. Within mainstream design logic, multi-branch methods require extra supervision and annotations, while streamlined designs prioritize low-level visual saliency over true gaze intent. The former leads to a high annotation burden and hinders domain transfer, whereas the latter causes misalignment between predicted attention and actual gaze targets. To address this issue, we propose TextGaze, a unified cross-modal architecture that leverages a Large Vision-Language Model (LVLM) as scalable semantic guidance to balance the two design paradigms. The model extracts visual features from a frozen encoder and utilizes an LVLM to obtain gaze-aligned textual cues. We design a transformer-based fusion module with hierarchical text supervision to preserve task semantics. Lightweight decoding heads enable the joint prediction of gaze heatmaps and in-/out-of-frame status. We evaluate our method on four mainstream datasets, and the results show competitive performance across key metrics with robust cross-dataset generalisation without extra fine-tuning. Overall, we provide a streamlined alternative to traditional designs and highlight the potential of LVLMs as accessible auxiliary guidance for gaze estimation.

</details>


### [36] [FlowPainter: Inpainting Optical Flow via Confidence-Guided Completion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.10140)
*Yuang Meng,Chenyang Wu,Xianshun Liu,Chun-Le Guo,Zichen Liang,Lina Lei,Jie Liang,Hui Zeng,Chongyi Li,Lei Zhang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: FlowPainter 提出一种置信度引导的扩散式光流框架，通过轻量网络预测粗糙光流和置信度掩码，将简单区域的可靠运动作为先验注入扩散去噪过程，加速收敛并提升在复杂运动区域的精度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有扩散光流方法对整个密集光流场从高斯噪声去噪，包括简单区域（轻量网络即可可靠估计），增加了去噪负担，导致收敛慢、训练不稳定。

Method: 提出FlowPainter框架：1) 轻量置信度感知网络预测粗糙光流和像素级置信度掩码；2) 以简单区域光流作为先验进行置信度初始化；3) 通过置信度门控残差指导注入迭代去噪；4) 动态衰减指导强度，稳定早期去噪并保留后期精化能力。

Result: 在Sintel、KITTI、Spring公开基准上，FlowPainter在可比训练设置下达到强精度，收敛效率优于现有扩散光流方法，在困难数据分割上尤其显著。

Conclusion: FlowPainter有效融合了判别式先验与扩散精化，为光流估计提供了一种实用且高效的范式。

Abstract: Existing optical flow methods broadly follow two paradigms: iterative optimization and diffusion-based estimation. Iterative methods, exemplified by RAFT, achieve high accuracy through recurrent refinement, but remain challenged by large displacements and complex motion. Diffusion-based methods introduce generative modeling and show promise in such ambiguous regions. However, existing diffusion models usually denoise the entire dense flow field from Gaussian noise, including simple regions where reliable motion can already be estimated by a lightweight network. This increases the denoising burden and may cause slow convergence and unstable training. To address this issue, we introduce FlowPainter, a diffusion-based optical flow framework that reformulates dense-flow generation as confidence-guided soft inpainting. FlowPainter employs a lightweight confidence-aware network to predict a rough flow and a pixel-wise confidence mask, distinguishing reliable simple regions from uncertain hard regions. The resulting simple-flow prior is used for confidence-based initialization and further injected into iterative denoising through confidence-gated residual guidance. With dynamically decaying guidance strength, FlowPainter stabilizes early denoising while preserving the flexibility of the diffusion model for late-stage detail refinement. Extensive experiments on public benchmarks, including Sintel, KITTI, and Spring, show that FlowPainter achieves strong accuracy under comparable training settings and converges more efficiently than existing diffusion-based optical flow methods, with notable gains on challenging benchmark splits. Our approach offers a practical way to integrate reliable discriminative priors with diffusion-based refinement for optical flow estimation. Our code is publicly available at https://github.com/mya012/FlowPainter.

</details>


### [37] [REVA-PO: Stabilizing Reinforcement Learning for Chest X-ray Report Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.10147)
*Li Guo,Anas M. Tahir,Z. Jane Wang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出REVA-PO框架，通过响应加权正则化（RER）和验证锚定策略重置（VAPR）稳定强化学习训练，在胸部X光报告生成任务上达到SOTA。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有RL训练因固定KL正则化和静态参考策略导致不稳定、探索受限、KL压力累积，亟需更稳定的训练框架。

Method: 1) 响应加权正则化（RER）：根据优势和参考策略熵动态调整每条响应的KL权重；2) 验证锚定策略重置（VAPR）：定期将参考策略与当前策略同步到最佳验证检查点；3) 三阶段流水线：预热训练→分类器引导的监督微调→强化学习。

Result: MIMIC-CXR上BLEU-4提升5.1%，IU-Xray上提升3.6%；CheXpert F1提升4.5%，RadGraph F1提升12.8%。

Conclusion: REVA-PO在语言质量和临床准确性上均达到新SOTA，有效解决了RL训练的不稳定性和探索受限问题。

Abstract: Automated chest X-ray report generation has recently benefited from reinforcement learning (RL) and large language models. However, RL training often suffers from instability or limited exploration due to fixed Kullback-Leibler (KL) regularization and a static reference policy that accumulates KL pressure over time. We propose Response-Weighted and Validation-Anchored Policy Optimization (REVA-PO), a RL framework that stabilizes long-term training via Response-Weighted Regularization (RER) and Validation-Anchored Policy Reset (VAPR). RER dynamically adjusts per-response KL weights based on advantage and reference-policy entropy, relaxing constraints for high-quality responses while tightening them for low-quality ones. Complementarily, VAPR periodically synchronizes the reference and current policies to the best validation checkpoint, resetting accumulated regularization pressure to expand the viable exploration space.
