Unlike standard Multi-Head Self Attention (MSA), WS-MoH generates a representation for each 3D window and dynamically routes the entire window to specialized or common attention heads. This enables spatially adaptive feature extraction tailored to the local context of each window, enhancing specialization and reducing redundancy without increasing parameters. Evaluated on a retrospective dataset of 168 T1-weighted MRI scans, MMA-Former achieved an AUC of 0.752, outperforming other 3D architectures, including the best CNN (AUC of 0.708) and Transformer baselines (AUC of 0.681).

</details>


### [111] [TreeSoc: Tree-Structured Dynamic Reasoning and Tool Synergy for Soccer Video Understanding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.10990)
*Thanh-Nhan Vo,Thanh-Khoi Nguyen,Trong-Thuan Nguyen,Trung-Hoang Le,Minh-Triet Tran*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: TreeSoc提出了一种基于树状结构分层推理的框架，将足球视频问答转化为深度优先搜索问题，通过动态子任务分解和自适应工具路由，在多个基准上取得SOTA性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有视觉语言模型在处理复杂足球场景视频时存在浅层跨模态对齐、多步推理能力不足以及工具集成受限等问题，难以应对复杂的视频问答任务。

Method: 采用动态深度优先搜索（DFS）机制，将复杂查询分解为有序子任务，通过树状结构进行迭代推理，并在每个推理节点自适应激活领域专用模块，整合多源信息生成上下文相关的预测。

Result: 在SoccerBench上，TextQA、ImageQA、VideoQA分别达到85.2%、87.4%和82.2%的准确率；在NExT-QA上取得74.16%的准确率，均达到当前最优水平。

Conclusion: 结构化的工具增强树推理是鲁棒视频理解的有效范式，能够显著提升复杂场景下的推理能力。

Abstract: Automated understanding of complex soccer scenarios from video remains a significant challenge for contemporary vision-language models (VLMs), which suffer from shallow cross-modal alignment and exhibit fundamental limitations in multi-step reasoning and coordinated tool integration. We present TreeSoc, a structured reasoning framework that reformulates soccer video question answering as a hierarchical search problem rather than a single-pass prediction. Specifically, TreeSoc employs a dynamic depth-first search (DFS) mechanism that decomposes complex queries into sequentially ordered sub-tasks, enabling iterative reasoning refinement through explicit intermediate states. This tree-structured decomposition naturally supports adaptive tool routing, wherein domain-specific modules are selectively activated and their outputs incorporated at each reasoning node to produce contextually grounded predictions. On SoccerBench, TreeSoc achieves state-of-the-art performance, with accuracies of 85.2%, 87.4%, and 82.2% on TextQA, ImageQA, and VideoQA, respectively. Additionally, TreeSoc further demonstrates strong cross-domain generalization, attaining 74.16% accuracy on NExT-QA. These results establish structured, tool-augmented tree reasoning as an effective paradigm for robust video understanding. Code is available at: https://github.com/thanhnhan29/TreeSoc.

</details>


### [112] [LoSA-Net: A Localized and Scale-Adaptive Network for Boundary-Sensitive Prediction of Perineural Invasion in 3D MRI](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.10992)
*Youngung Han,Hyunsu Go,Kyeonghun Kim,Induk Um,Junga Kim,Jaewon Jung,Woo Kyoung Jeong,Won Jae Lee,Pa Hong,Ken Ying-Kai Liao,Hyuk-Jae Lee,Nam-Joon Kim*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: LoSA-Net是一种用于3D MRI中神经侵犯（PNI）预测的局部化、尺度自适应架构，通过TNA、SAFM和CSRA三个模块解决传统模型在细粒度边界感知上的不足，在胆管癌数据集上达到0.7567的AUC，优于现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: PNI是肿瘤侵袭性的临床相关指标，影响手术决策，但MRI中PNI的细微特征常与周围解剖结构相似，传统下采样或全局特征聚合会削弱这些细粒度神经周围线索，因此需要更可靠的术前无创评估方法。

Method: 提出LoSA-Net，包含三个核心模块：1) Talking Neighborhood Attention (TNA) 通过局部化自注意力与头间混合保留神经对齐细节；2) Scale-Adaptive Feature Mixing (SAFM) 利用多尺度深度可分离处理调整感受野；3) Cross-Scale Refinement and Alignment (CSRA) 在跨阶段间保持语义上下文与高分辨率边界的一致性。

Result: 在168例胆管癌患者的增强MRI扫描中，LoSA-Net达到0.7567的AUC，在相同的预处理和优化设置下优于代表性卷积和Transformer基线模型。

Conclusion: LoSA-Net通过局部化且尺度自适应的设计有效提升了PNI预测性能，验证了在细粒度边界感知任务中的有效性。

Abstract: Perineural invasion (PNI) is a clinically relevant indicator of tumor aggressiveness and can influence surgical decision-making, motivating interest in reliable preoperative assessment. The subtle MRI features of PNI, however, often resemble nearby anatomy, complicating noninvasive prediction. These fine perineural cues are easily attenuated by routine downsampling or overly global feature aggregation, reducing the effectiveness of conventional volumetric models. We present LoSA-Net, a localized and scale-adaptive architecture for boundary-sensitive PNI prediction in 3D MRI. Talking Neighborhood Attention (TNA) preserves nerve-aligned detail through localized self-attention with head-wise mixing, and Scale-Adaptive Feature Mixing (SAFM) modulates the receptive field using multi-scale depthwise processing. Cross-Scale Refinement and Alignment (CSRA) maintains consistency between semantic context and high-resolution boundaries across stages. In contrast-enhanced MRI scans from 168 patients with cholangiocarcinoma, LoSA-Net achieves an AUC of 0.7567 and outperforms representative convolutional and transformer baselines under matched preprocessing and optimization settings.

</details>


### [113] [Confidence Scores in Open-Vocabulary Detection Are a Biased Mixture of Scale and Semantics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.10993)
*Yi Tang Soon,Jun-Wei Hsieh*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 基于CLIP等基础模型的开词汇检测器在目标检测中，其置信度分数存在两种系统性偏差：尺度偏差（大物体分数虚高）和语义偏差（通用查询分数被压制）。这些偏差源于CLIP的图像级预训练结构，无法通过简单阈值调整消除。提出的温度缩放校正方法能在不重新训练的情况下将小物体召回率提升19.6%，但会轻微降低排序精度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 揭示基于基础模型（如CLIP）的开词汇目标检测器中存在的置信度分数偏差问题，证明这些分数并非可靠的位置概率估计，而是混杂了视觉尺度和语义查询特异性的影响。

Method: 在COCO数据集上对三种检测器（GroundingDINO、OWL-ViT、YOLO-World）进行控制实验，并在LVIS（1203类别）上用GroundingDINO验证尺度偏差。通过统计分析（α=+0.064, r=0.579, p=1.29×10^-58）证明尺度偏差，通过语义偏差分析（β=-0.705, p=5.23×10^-41）证明语义偏差。提出参数无关的温度缩放校正方法。

Result: 尺度偏差使大物体分数系统性偏高；语义偏差压制通用查询的分数。oracle每尺度阈值调整下，小物体ΔF1=+0.001，大物体ΔF1=+0.102。温度缩放校正使小物体Recall@10提升19.6%（p<0.01），但会带来排序精度的微小损失。

Conclusion: 证明了将图像级基础模型（如CLIP）适配到区域级检测任务存在根本性局限，偏差部分可逆但需付出代价。

Abstract: Foundation models such as CLIP have enabled open-vocabulary object detectors that generalise to novel categories via vision-language similarity. However, the confidence scores these detectors produce are not reliable localization probability estimates: they conflate visual scale and semantic query specificity with the true detection signal. Through controlled experiments on COCO across three foundation-model-based detectors (GroundingDINO, OWL-ViT, YOLO-World), with the scale-bias finding further replicated on LVIS (1,203 categories) using GroundingDINO, we show that s=cos(v,t) is a biased mixture of two effects. Scale bias (alpha = +0.064, r = 0.579, p = 1.29 x 10^-58) systematically inflates scores for large objects. Semantic bias (beta = -0.705, p = 5.23 x 10^-41) suppresses scores for generic queries. Both biases are structurally inevitable from CLIP's image-level pretraining. Threshold adjustment cannot remove them: oracle per-scale thresholding yields Delta F1 = +0.001 for small objects versus +0.102 for large. A parameter-free temperature scaling correction improves small-object Recall@10 by 19.6% (p < 0.01) without retraining. This comes at a modest, measurable cost to pooled-ranking precision, so the bias is partially, not freely, reversible at inference time. These findings reveal a fundamental limitation of adapting image-level foundation models to region-level detection tasks.

</details>


### [114] [AsySplat: Efficient Asymmetric 3D Gaussian Splatting for Long-Sequence Scene Modeling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.10995)
*Yingji Zhong,Dave Zhenyu Chen,Fuzhao Ou,Youyu Chen,Zhihao Li,Lanqing Hong,Dan Xu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出非对称架构解耦几何与外观建模，在保持高质量新视角合成的同时大幅降低计算冗余，实现近800倍加速。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有可泛化3D高斯泼溅模型在长序列新视角合成中存在大量计算冗余，需要更高效的计算分配策略。

Method: 设计非对称双分支架构：几何分支处理粗粒度token、使用大部分参数进行多视图重建；外观分支处理细粒度token、使用少量参数捕捉细节；通过双边连接实现分支间相互引导。

Result: 在32视图960P输入上匹配基于优化的方法，实现近800倍加速；以更少参数和更低训练/推理开销超越SOTA可泛化模型的零样本性能。

Conclusion: 任务感知的非对称设计能有效减少计算冗余、提高参数效率，使小模型也能取得强性能，实现整体效率提升。

Abstract: Recent generalizable 3D Gaussian Splatting models have advanced long-sequence novel view synthesis (NVS), but at the cost of substantial redundant computation. We identify that the redundancy can be mitigated based on two observations: (i) high-precision geometry is not strictly required for high-quality NVS; (ii) appearance learning is generally easier than geometry recovery. Motivated by these insights, we propose an asymmetric architecture that decouples geometry and appearance modeling. The geometry branch processes coarse-grained tokens with most of the parameters for multi-view reconstruction, while the appearance branch operates on fine-grained tokens to capture details using significantly fewer parameters. The two branches interact through bilateral connections, enabling mutual guidance for their respective tasks. This task-aware asymmetry reduces the computational redundancy and allocates the computation more judiciously, thereby increasing parameter efficiency and enabling smaller models to achieve strong performance. On 32-view 960P inputs, our model matches optimization-based methods while delivering nearly 800x speedup, and surpasses the zero-shot performance of state-of-the-art generalizable models with markedly fewer parameters and reduced training/inference overhead, achieving an overall efficiency improvement.

</details>


### [115] [Temporal Feature Distillation for Label-Efficient Precise Event Spotting in Sports Videos](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.10998)
*Hao Xu,Xinyu Wei,Sam Wells,Sunil Aryal*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种针对精确事件定位（PES）的半监督方法——时间特征蒸馏（TFD），通过蒸馏骨干网络的时间敏感特征而非投影头输出，并结合Transformer门控移位模块（TGS）注入运动信息，在细粒度体育视频基准上显著提升了性能，仅用10%标注数据即可超越现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 精确事件定位（PES）需要区分视觉相似但语义不同的相邻帧，直接应用DINO等自蒸馏方法会导致运动线索被抑制为噪声，无法感知精确事件边界。

Method: 提出时间特征蒸馏（TFD），在骨干网络层面对齐时间信息丰富的特征以保留运动敏感和边界感知的线索；采用带斜坡上升调度的监督预热阶段稳定训练；提出Transformer门控移位模块（TGS），通过多尺度门控移位操作向ViT注入运动感知的时序信息。

Result: 在四个细粒度体育基准上，该方法一致优于全监督和半监督基线。在FSPerf上仅用10%标注数据，mAP提升4.54个百分点；仅用80%标注数据即可在两个数据集上匹配或超越全监督100%基线的表现。

Conclusion: 通过保留骨干网络中的运动敏感特征而非投影头输出，并引入运动感知的时序模块，半监督框架能有效解决PES中细粒度边界感知问题，显著减少对标注数据的依赖。

Abstract: Precise Event Spotting (PES) requires distinguishing visually similar yet semantically distinct adjacent frames, making it fundamentally different from image classification and coarse action recognition. Although self-distillation methods such as DINO have shown strong representation learning ability in images, we find that directly applying them to PES is ineffective: without supervised guidance, subtle but crucial motion cues are often suppressed as noise, leading to representations that are insensitive to precise event boundaries. To address this, we propose Temporal Feature Distillation, a semi-supervised objective that aligns temporally informative backbone features, rather than projection-head outputs, to preserve motion-sensitive and boundary-aware cues for frame-level localization. A supervised warm-up with a ramp-up schedule further stabilizes training by ensuring that meaningful event cues are learned before unlabeled distillation begins. We also introduce Transformer Gate Shift, a multi-scale gated shifting module that injects motion-aware temporal information into Vision Transformers. Experiments on four fine-grained sports benchmarks show consistent improvements over fully supervised and semi-supervised baselines. Under 10\% supervision on FSPerf, our method improves mAP by 4.54 points over the strongest competing approach, and with only 80\% labeled data, it matches or surpasses the fully supervised 100\% baseline on two of the four datasets.

</details>


### [116] [SynCLIP: Synonym-Coherent Language-Image Pretraining for Robust Open-Vocabulary Dense Perception](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.11008)
*Mingjie Xie,Guangjun He,Dongli Xu,Youtian Lin,Hongjue Li,Pengming Feng,Jian Guan,Yue Deng*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: SynCLIP通过语义一致的注意力对齐与优化模块，解决开放词汇密集感知中同义词引发的空间注意力不一致问题，在多个基准上达到SOTA。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有CLIP-based开放词汇密集感知方法存在同义词引发的空间注意力不一致问题，即语义等价的表达会产生不同的空间关注模式，影响鲁棒性和性能。

Method: 提出SynCLIP框架，包含：(1) 语义一致性空间注意力对齐(SSA)模块，最小化原始与同义表达间注意力图的差异；(2) 空间注意力细化(SAR)模块，选择性增强最相关空间区域；(3) 构建同义词增强视觉语料库(SEViC)，为每个类别扩充同义词和文本定义。

Result: 在多个基准测试上，SynCLIP显著提升了不同语言变体下的定位一致性，并在CLIP-based OVDP方法中达到最优性能。

Conclusion: 通过处理同义词引起的注意力不一致问题，SynCLIP有效增强了开放词汇密集感知的鲁棒性和准确性，代码已开源。

Abstract: Open-vocabulary dense perception (OVDP) aims to localize objects unseen during training by leveraging textual knowledge. Despite the remarkable progress of recent CLIP-based approaches, we identify a critical limitation: synonym-induced grounding inconsistency, where semantically equivalent expressions yield disparate spatial attention patterns. This inconsistency undermines the robustness and performance of existing methods in real-world OVDP applications. To address this issue, we propose SynCLIP, a Synonym-Coherent Language-Image Pretraining framework that enhances synonym-robust grounding for OVDP. SynCLIP introduces a Semantic-consistent Spatial Attention alignment (SSA) module to enhance spatial attention consistency by minimizing discrepancies between attention maps of original and synonymous expressions. Furthermore, a Spatial Attention Refinement (SAR) module selectively strengthens the most semantically relevant spatial regions within aligned maps for more precise and stable grounding. To support synonym-coherent pretraining, we also construct a Synonym-Enriched Visual Corpus (SEViC), which augments each category with multiple synonyms and textual definitions. Extensive experiments on multiple benchmarks demonstrate that SynCLIP substantially improves grounding consistency under diverse linguistic variants and achieves state-of-the-art performance among CLIP-based OVDP methods. Code is available at https://github.com/Justlovesmile/SynCLIP.

</details>


### [117] [Reference-Based Face Super-Resolution Using the Spatial Transformer](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.11025)
*Varun Ramesh Jois,Antonella DiLillo,James Storer*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出一种基于参考图像的人脸超分辨率方法，通过空间变换器对齐模块和自适应聚合函数，在较小模型上实现了多个数据集的最优效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 人脸超分辨率是计算机视觉中的常见任务，但作为病态问题，高保真重建极具挑战。

Method: 采用基于参考图像的方法，使用空间变换器（而非可变形卷积）进行稳定对齐，并设计了一种聚合函数，能在参考图像质量好时提取有效信息、质量差时抑制其作用。

Result: 该模型规模较小，但在多个数据集上达到了最先进的性能。

Conclusion: 基于空间变换器的对齐模块比可变形卷积更稳定，所提聚合函数能灵活处理参考图像质量变化，证明了小模型也能实现高精度人脸超分辨率。

Abstract: Face super-resolution is the task of increasing the resolution of an image containing a face thereby adding finer detail. It is a ubiquitous task in many computer vision applications and quite often the user isn't even aware that it is being performed. However, doing it with high fidelity is challenging as it is an ill-posed problem. In this paper we present a reference-based solution for face super-resolution that uses higher resolution reference images to aid in the task. We show an alignment module based on the spatial transformer that is considerably more stable than the popular deformable convolutions. We also show an aggregation function that can take good quality information from the reference images when available or suppress the function when such information is unavailable. Finally, we show that our relatively smaller model can achieve state of the art results on multiple datasets. The source code is available at https://github.com/varun-jois/FSRST.

</details>


### [118] [RTFVE: Realtime Face Video Enhancement](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.11034)
*Varun Ramesh Jois,Antonella DiLillo,James Storer*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出了一种名为RTFVE的实时人脸视频增强模型，该模型可轻松集成到任何视频解码器中，在普通CPU上实时运行，能有效提升低比特率视频的感知质量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有视频增强深度学习模型要么难以与现代压缩标准集成，要么需要GPU等专用硬件才能运行，实用性差。用户普遍面临带宽限制，低比特率视频质量亟待提升。

Method: 提出RTFVE模型，设计上可方便地集成到任何视频解码器中，且无需专用GPU，在普通CPU上即可实现实时推理。

Result: 在多种低比特率设置下，该模型相比压缩视频基线显著提升了感知质量。

Conclusion: RTFVE模型在兼顾实时性与硬件普适性的前提下，有效改善了低比特率视频的人脸区域画质，具有实用应用价值。

Abstract: There's been a surge in adoption of video conferencing applications for both personal and business use cases. However, the bandwidth limitations faced by many users worldwide may restrict the optimal use of such applications. Although deep learning offers a solution for enhancing low bit rate videos, most models today are either hard to incorporate with modern compression standards or require specialized hardware to run such as significant GPUs making these models impractical. To address these issues, we introduce the Realtime Face Video Enhancement (RTFVE) model which can be easily incorporated with any video decoder and can run in realtime on ordinary CPUs. Experiments show that our model improves perceptual quality over the compressed video baseline at multiple low bitrate settings. The source code will be made available at https://github.com/varun-jois/RTFVE.

</details>


### [119] [FSFVE: Few Shot Compressed Face Video Enhancement](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.11040)
*Varun Ramesh Jois,Antonella DiLillo,James Storer*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出一种轻量级框架，能在100秒内基于10帧人脸图像训练模型，实时增强低带宽视频通话质量，无需修改现有通话应用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管大多数用户拥有必要硬件，仍有数百万人在低带宽条件下体验质量低下的视频通话，需要一种不依赖修改现有应用的增强方案。

Method: 基于10帧人脸图像快速（<100秒）训练一个轻量化模型，可在通话前或通话中完成训练；系统作为中间层拦截并改善Zoom等现成视频应用显示的图像，无需修改应用本身，并在低计算设备（如笔记本电脑CPU）上实时运行。

Result: 实验表明该方法在量化指标和感知质量上均显著提升了压缩人脸视频的清晰度。

Conclusion: 该框架能快速、高效地增强低带宽视频通话质量，兼容现有应用，具有实用价值。

Abstract: Videocalling has become a popular form of communication in the world today, with many companies providing free services for it. However, there are still millions of people around the world that experience poor quality videocalls due to limitations in bandwidth. This despite, most people having the required hardware. In this paper we present a novel framework for enhancing highly compressed videocalls. We show, that with as little as 10 frames of the face, we can rapidly (in under 100 seconds) train a model to enhance that instance of the videocall. The model can be trained either prior to or during the call, enhancing the rest of the call by producing better quality video. The video conferencing application need not be modified - it can be off the shelf with our system as a layer on top that trains quickly then simply lets the video conferencing application (e.g. Zoom) run as usual, where our system intercepts and improves images before they are displayed. The model is designed to run in realtime on low-compute devices such as a typical laptop CPU. Experimentally, we show that the model significantly improves quality of compressed face video both quantitatively as well as perceptually. Code can be found at https://github.com/varun-jois/FSFVE.

</details>


### [120] [WiFi-JEPA: Self-supervised Learning for WiFi-CSI 3D Human Pose Estimation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.11064)
*Doeon Kim,Jungyoon Lee,Seongsin Kim,Seong-heum Kim*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: WiFi-JEPA是一种自监督学习框架，通过预测掩码潜变量而非重建原始CSI信号，结合射线追踪仿真数据，实现了在环境迁移下鲁棒的WiFi三维人体姿态估计，显著优于现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有WiFi姿态估计器在环境变化时容易失效，且依赖昂贵的摄像头标注流程，限制了数据规模的扩展。

Method: 提出CSI专用令牌化和链路掩码策略，通过射线追踪仿真生成多样化无标注CSI数据进行预训练，并采用掩码潜变量预测方式学习CSI原生表征。

Result: 在Person-in-WiFi-3D数据集上，WiFi-JEPA在单人和多人3D姿态估计任务中均达到最优性能；仿真CSI数据可与真实CSI互补；四种视觉自监督学习目标均导致性能下降，而WiFi-JEPA持续提升下游任务表现。

Conclusion: WiFi-JEPA通过自监督学习有效克服了环境迁移问题，射线追踪仿真为大规模无标注预训练提供了可行路径，为WiFi感知领域树立了新基准。

Abstract: WiFi Channel State Information (CSI) enables privacy-preserving human pose sensing in camera-denied environments, but existing WiFi-based pose estimators often fail under environment shifts and rely on costly camera-based annotation pipelines that limit scale. We propose WiFi-JEPA, a self-supervised framework that learns CSI-native representations by predicting masked latent embeddings instead of reconstructing raw CSI signals that may contain hardware-specific artifacts. WiFi-JEPA makes three contributions: (i) CSI-specific tokenization and link masking tailored to the CSI tensor over channel, time, and link (C,T,L); masking entire Tx-Rx antenna links forces the model to predict one spatial link view from others, capturing cross-link correlations informative of 3D spatial structure. (ii) A ray-tracing CSI simulation pipeline that generates diverse unlabeled CSI from randomized geometric primitives, providing scalable pre-training data without pose annotations. (iii) State-of-the-art results on Person-in-WiFi-3D: WiFi-JEPA outperforms prior WiFi-CSI baselines on both single- and multi-person 3D pose estimation under the same evaluation protocol. We also show that simulated CSI provides complementary pre-training signal to real CSI, and that four vision-native SSL objectives degrade performance below training from scratch, whereas WiFi-JEPA consistently improves downstream pose estimation.

</details>


### [121] [DDR-Net: Haze-Aware Dual-Domain Refinement for Single-Image Dehazing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.11071)
*Xinye Zheng,Ye Yu,Qiang Lu,Jinsheng Luo,Yiran Cui,Yongbin Cheng*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出了一种基于 haze-aware 双域精炼网络（DDR-Net）用于单幅图像去雾，通过雾先验提取、细节增强块和空间-频率瓶颈精炼三个模块，在真实数据集上优于现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有端到端去雾网络存在两个问题：瓶颈阶段特征精炼不足以及编码器-解码器结构中局部结构表示能力弱，限制了去雾效果。

Method: 提出 DDR-Net，包含三个核心模块：(1) Haze Prior Extractor (HPE) 从下采样雾图提取多尺度雾感知先验；(2) Detail-Enhanced Blocks (DE Blocks) 通过梯度感知卷积增强边缘和纹理恢复；(3) Spatial-Frequency Bottleneck Refinement (SFBR) 在瓶颈处联合利用空间和频率信息进行特征精炼。

Result: 在真实世界基准数据集上优于现有去雾方法，在合成数据集上也取得了有竞争力的性能。

Conclusion: DDR-Net 通过多尺度雾先验提取、细节增强和空间-频率双域精炼，实现了更有效的特征表示和重建，提升了去雾效果。

Abstract: Single-image dehazing aims to recover clear scenes from haze-degraded images. It remains challenging due to the atmospheric scattering and the complexity of real-world haze distributions. Although recent end-to-end networks have achieved promising performance, two issues still limit their effectiveness: insufficient feature refinement at the bottleneck stage and weak local structural representation in encoder-decoder architectures. Thus, we propose a Haze-Aware Dual-Domain Refinement Network (DDR-Net) for single-image dehazing. Our method is built upon three modules: Haze Prior Extractor (HPE) provides multi-scale haze-aware priors by operating directly on downsampled hazy images; Detail-Enhanced Blocks (DE Blocks) serve as the core feature extraction units, capturing multi-scale structural information and enhancing edge and texture recovery via gradient-aware convolutions; and Spatial-Frequency Bottleneck Refinement (SFBR) at the bottleneck jointly exploits spatial and frequency information to refine bottleneck features. DDR-Net achieves more effective feature representation and reconstruction for haze removal. Extensive experiments on real-world benchmarks demonstrate that our method outperforms existing dehazing approaches. It achieves competitive performance on synthetic datasets.

</details>


### [122] [Do Video-LLMs Actually Watch? Diagnosing Character-Tracking Failures in Long-Form Video](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.11078)
*Mohammad Al-Ratrout,Shayla Sharmin,Aditya Raikwar,Roghayeh Leila Barmaki*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文发现当前视频大语言模型（Video-LLM）在角色跟踪任务上的高分并非源于真正的角色识别能力，而是依赖于粗浅的性别线索和选项猜测。即使换掉问题中的人物名字，模型改变答案的比例仅为4-31%，说明它们基本忽略了问题询问的对象。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有视频大语言模型在角色跟踪基准测试（如InfiniBench的全局外观任务）中达到37-38%的准确率，但研究者质疑这一分数究竟来自真实的角色跟踪能力，还是来自更简单的捷径。

Method: 设计了一个九种条件的诊断测试协议，应用于三种架构不同的开源视频大语言模型（以Gemini 2.5 Flash作为前沿参考），包括：1）更换问题中的人物名字（分同性别和跨性别两种情况）；2）将多选题改为开放式问题；3）添加字幕、使用信息最丰富的帧、加倍帧数等控制实验。

Result: 1）更换问题人名后，模型仅4-31%改变答案；2）跨性别更换时反应更大（13-28个百分点的差距），说明模型只能识别粗略的性别线索，无法区分同性别个体；3）开放式问题下开源模型准确率下降18-25个百分点，151个回答无一完全正确；4）添加字幕、选择信息帧、加倍帧数均未改善角色跟踪能力。

Conclusion: 当前视频大语言模型的基准测试高分并非来自真实的角色跟踪能力，瓶颈不在于模型看到多少视频内容，而在于如何将视频与问题中指定的人物关联起来。论文发布了用于审计基准测试分数的诊断工具包。

Abstract: Can a Video Large Language Model (Video-LLM) follow one person through a long video, keeping track of who they are well enough to report, in order, how their outfit changes across a full TV episode? Benchmarks increasingly score this kind of task, and the strongest open-source 7--8B models now reach 37--38% on InfiniBench's global appearance task, which asks exactly that. But does that score come from tracking the named character, or from something easier? We test this with a nine-condition diagnostic protocol applied to three architecturally distinct open-source Video-LLMs, with Gemini~2.5~Flash as a frontier reference, and find the accuracy does not come from character tracking. When we change the character named in the question to a different cast member, leaving the video and answer options untouched, the models change their answer only 4--31% of the time, so they are largely ignoring who the question asks about. Breaking that test down by the gender of the swapped name shows why: the models react more when the name is changed to a different-gender character than to a same-gender one (a 13--28 point gap), picking up coarse gender cues but unable to tell same-gender individuals apart. This shallow processing surfaces again when we drop the multiple-choice options and ask the same questions open-endedly: open-source accuracy drops 18--25 points, with none of 151 answers fully correct, versus a 12-point drop for Gemini. Further checks rule out the obvious innocent explanations, adding subtitles, using the most informative frames, or doubling the number of frames all leave character tracking unimproved, so the bottleneck is not how much video the model sees but how it ties that video to the person the question names. We release a diagnostic toolkit for auditing what such benchmark scores actually measure.

</details>


### [123] [Controlling Motion Transfer in Diffusion Transformers via Attention Heads](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.11081)
*Sunyoung Jung,Jiwoo Park,Yoonseok Choi,Kyobin Choo,Ming-Hsuan Yang,Seong Jae Hwang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于注意力头部分析的无参数更新视频运动迁移框架，通过识别并操控DiTs中负责运动和结构的特定注意力头，实现了精确的运动迁移和结构保持。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: DiTs在视频生成中表现优异，但在运动迁移任务中面临挑战，因为对运动与结构表征的理解有限，难以在遵循参考运动的同时对齐目标提示。

Method: 在注意力头级别分析视频DiTs，识别出专门负责运动和空间结构的头部；通过语义对应引导优化运动专用头的运动线索，并通过选择性特征注入保留结构信息，整个框架无需参数更新。

Result: 实现了精确的运动迁移，同时为基于DiTs的可控视频生成提供了可解释的基础。

Conclusion: 本文证明了注意力头级别的控制能够有效实现视频运动迁移，为理解DiTs内部运动与结构表征提供了新视角。

Abstract: Diffusion Transformers (DiTs) have advanced video generation with high-quality, temporally coherent results. However, extending them to motion transfer, which requires following reference motion while aligning with a target prompt, remains challenging due to limited understanding of motion and structure representations within DiTs. We analyze video DiTs at the attention-head level and identify distinct heads specialized for motion and spatial structure. Based on this insight, we propose a head-aware controllable motion transfer framework that requires no parameter updates. Our method refines motion cues from motion-specialized heads via semantic correspondence guidance and preserves structure through selective feature injection. This head-level control not only enables accurate motion transfer but also provides an interpretable foundation for controllable video generation with DiTs.

</details>


### [124] [CUST: Clustered Unit-level Similarity Transformer for Lightweight Image Super-Resolution](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.11088)
*Jeongsoo Kim*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出了一种名为CUST的新型Transformer架构，通过聚类单元级相似性注意力机制，在保证全局-局部信息融合的同时显著降低计算复杂度，实现了高效的图像超分辨率重建。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有ViT模型在图像超分辨率中因二次计算复杂度导致高延迟和大内存消耗；而窗口注意力机制虽降低复杂度，但牺牲了长程依赖建模能力。需要一种能兼顾效率与全局信息捕获的新方法。

Method: (1) 聚类单元级相似性Transformer：让每个patch在局部窗口外聚合并关注相似patch，扩展感受野；(2) 重叠注意力窗口捕捉局部依赖；(3) 通过原始特征与其下采样-上采样后的残差显式提取高频细节。

Result: 实现了计算效率与恢复性能的平衡，在真实约束下内存占用更低、推理速度更快，优于最新的全局上下文或轻量级模型。

Conclusion: CUST通过创新的聚类相似性机制，在不牺牲全局建模能力的前提下显著降低ViT开销，为高效图像超分辨率提供了新思路。

Abstract: Recently, Vision Transformer (ViT)-based models have exhibited remarkable performance in image super-resolution. However, the quadratic computational complexity of ViTs with respect to spatial resolution severely constrains their efficiency, leading to high latency and massive memory consumption. To alleviate this, various window-based attention mechanisms have been proposed; yet, they inherently compromise the long-range dependency modeling that is the primary advantage of ViTs. To overcome these limitations, we propose the Clustered Unit-level Similarity Transformer (CUST), a novel architecture that efficiently integrates global and local information. Specifically, CUST enables each patch to aggregate and attend to similar patches within a broadened regional scope outside its local window, thereby capturing extensive contextual understanding. Furthermore, it employs overlapping attention windows to capture local dependencies, while explicitly extracting high-frequency details by computing the residual difference between the original features and their downsampled-upsampled counterparts. Comprehensive experiments demonstrate that our proposed model achieves a practical balance between computational efficiency and restoration performance. It achieves a lower memory footprint and faster inference speed compared to recent global context or lightweight models under realistic constraints. Code is available at [https://github.com/jwgdmkj/CUST].

</details>


### [125] [Why Low-Light Cameras Go Color Blind: Removing Color Bias in Raw Denoising](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.11090)
*Mohammad Mohammadi,Sina Honari,Stavros Tsogkas,Tristan Aumentado-Armstrong,Michael S. Brown,Iqbal Mohomed,Konstantinos G. Derpanis,Alex Levinshtein,Igor Gilitschenski*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种无需相机校准的低光照原始图像去噪方法，通过预测黑电平误差来纠正颜色偏差，在多个数据集上表现优异，并发现了SIDD数据集中的颜色偏差问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有数据驱动的去噪方法依赖大规模成对数据，成本高；参数化噪声模型需要精确相机校准，对于未知设备不实用。因此需要一种相机无关、无需校准的低光照原始图像去噪方案。

Method: 识别出黑电平误差导致的颜色偏差是性能下降的主因，提出一个偏差估计网络，将黑电平误差作为噪声输入的全局特征进行预测，从而实现颜色校正。

Result: 在ELD、SID和LRID数据集上，该方法在盲去噪器中表现优越，尤其在颜色校正方面突出，许多情况下与更强监督方法竞争甚至超越。同时发现SIDD数据集存在颜色偏差问题，并提出了新的GT提取框架。

Conclusion: 提出的校准无关范式有效解决了低光照原始去噪中的颜色偏差问题，且揭示了现有数据集的质量问题，为后续研究提供了更可靠的基准。

Abstract: Raw images inherently suffer from noise due to the stochastic nature of light and sensor hardware imperfections. As real photon counts fall, the ratio of this noise to the signal degrades; consequently, for low-light conditions, robust denoising is especially vital for high-quality results. While recent data-driven methods achieve strong performance, they typically rely on large-scale noisy-clean image pairs that are costly and difficult to collect. Alternatively, parametric noise models can generate synthetic training data, but this necessitates precise camera calibration, which is often impractical for unknown devices. In this work, we propose a camera-agnostic, calibration-free paradigm for low-light raw denoising. We identify that color bias from black-level error is a primary source of performance degradation and causes severe color shifts. To mitigate this, we introduce a bias estimator network that predicts the black-level error as a global feature of the noisy input. We evaluate our approach across the ELD, SID, and LRID datasets, demonstrating superior performance among blind denoisers, particularly in terms of color correction. In many cases, we are competitive with-or can even surpass-methods with stronger supervision. Furthermore, we reveal that the widely used SIDD dataset contains significant color bias in its ground-truth images, which yields unrealistic color reproduction in trained models. We introduce a new ground-truth extraction framework to resolve this issue and provide a benchmark of existing methods on the corrected dataset.

</details>


### [126] [Difference-Driven Gating: Adaptive Feature Fusion for U-Net Decoder](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.11096)
*Kai Li,Xuechao Zou,Jiashen Fu,Zijun Yan,Xintong Wang,Xiaolin Hu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出两种基于差异的门控机制（FDG和EDG），用于U-Net风格模型中全局与局部特征的融合，在医学图像分割、遥感图像去云和语音分离任务上优于现有注意力融合方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有注意力融合方法通常仅从全局解码特征或全局与局部特征的相关性中推导注意力权重，忽略了两个特征流之间的差异信息。

Method: 提出两种差异门控方法：FDG直接使用全局与局部特征的绝对差异生成自适应门控图；EDG通过信息熵衡量各特征流的表征确定性，利用符号化的熵差推导注意力权重。两种方法均产生耦合门控图，同时调控全局和局部特征。

Result: 在医学图像分割、遥感图像去云和语音分离任务上，FDG和EDG均优于现有注意力融合方法，其中EDG表现更优。

Conclusion: 基于特征差异的门控机制为U-Net风格结构中的多尺度特征融合提供了新的有效范式。

Abstract: The U-Net style models have been widely used in many applications. A critical step in these models is to reconstruct the lower-level features using a top-down decoder. This reconstruction requires precise fusion of high-level semantics and low-level details. Existing attention-based fusion methods typically derive attention weights from the top-down decoder features (global) alone or the correlation between the top-down decoder features and the bottom-up encoder features (local), then modulate the encoder features using these weights. In this work, we explore a different paradigm: deriving attention weights from the difference between the two feature streams. To this end, we propose two difference-based gating approaches: Feature-difference gating (FDG), which directly uses the absolute difference between global and local features to generate adaptive gating maps, and Entropy-difference gating (EDG), which measures the representational certainty of each stream via information entropy and uses their signed entropy difference to derive the attention weights. Both methods produce coupled gating maps that simultaneously modulate the global and local features. Experiments on different tasks including medical image segmentation, remote sensing image cloud removal and speech separation showed that both methods outperformed existing attention-based fusion methods, and EDG performed better. The results suggested a new paradigm for multi-scale feature fusion in the U-Net style structures.

</details>


### [127] [Revisiting Matching Response and Swept Feature Volumes for Wide-baseline Omnidirectional Stereo](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.11097)
*Seungjin Jeon,Jongwoo Lim,Changhee Won*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种面向全向立体视觉的置信度估计训练策略，通过重新解释3D编码器-解码器产生的匹配响应，利用期望值作为内在置信度信号，无需辅助模块即可惩罚模糊匹配。同时引入扫描特征体素重采样方法，利用3D CNN的特征经正匹配索引重采样后由2D CNN预测表面法线等元信息，实现联合学习与几何正则化，提升了深度一致性与部署实用性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 全向立体视觉在宽基线设置下常出现模糊匹配问题，现有置信度估计方法需依赖辅助头、多次推理或额外模块，效率低且泛化性差。作者希望设计一种更高效、无需额外模块的置信度估计训练策略，并同时提升深度图的一致性。

Method: 1) 重新解释3D编码器-解码器的匹配响应，利用其期望值作为内在置信度信号，直接惩罚模糊匹配。2) 提出扫描特征体素重采样（Swept Feature Volume Resampling），将3D CNN生成的特征响应通过回归的正匹配索引进行重采样，再交由2D CNN预测表面法线等元信息，实现联合学习与几何正则化。

Result: 所提方法在置信度估计和表面法线预测任务上均取得性能提升，同时保持了在自动驾驶等移动应用中的部署实用性。

Conclusion: 通过将置信度信号内置于匹配响应中，并联合学习表面法线等几何元信息，能够在高效推理的同时提升深度一致性与泛化能力，适合实际部署。

Abstract: In this paper, we propose a training strategy for confidence estimation in omnidirectional stereo, targeting the ambiguous matches that frequently occur in wide-baseline setups. Reinterpreting the matching responses produced by the 3D encoder decoder block, we show that their expectation values provide intrinsic confidence signals. Building on this, our method directly penalizes ambiguous responses without auxiliary heads, multi-pass inference, or additional modules, resulting in more efficient and generalized predictions. Beyond confidence, we introduce swept feature volume resampling, where response features produced by 3D CNNs are resampled using regressed positive matching indices and then processed by 2D CNNs to predict meta-information such as surface normals. This joint learning introduces auxiliary geometric regularization and improves depth coherence by leveraging additional contextual cues during response aggregation stage. Experimental results demonstrate that our approach enhances both confidence estimation and surface normal prediction while maintaining deployment practicality for autonomous mobility applications.

</details>


### [128] [FlowPET: Physics-Informed Symplectic Flow Matching for Low-Count PET Reconstruction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.11104)
*Zheng Zhang,Hao Tang,Yingying Hu,Zhanli Hu,Jing Qin*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: FlowPET提出了一种基于辛相空间体积保持传输的PET重建方法，通过可分离哈密顿系统保证无散度向量场，从根本上防止弱信号的概率质量坍缩，在低计数PET重建中显著优于现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 低计数PET重建中，现有生成模型的耗散特性会导致相空间收缩，使诊断关键的低对比度病灶信号数值上"被洗掉"（概率质量坍缩），这是一个几何层面的根本性限制。

Method: 1) 将重建重新表述为辛相空间中的体积保持传输；2) 通过可分离哈密顿系统参数化后验动力学，构造无散度向量场；3) 引入基于PET算子Range-Null空间分解的共轭边界条件，在范围空间严格保证数据一致性，在零空间注入随机不确定性；4) 使用辛流匹配训练，辛蛙跳积分器推理。

Result: 在BrainWeb、临床儿科和UDPET数据集上，FlowPET在SSIM和PSNR上超越了最先进的确定性和随机基线方法，并展现出对低对比度病灶的优越恢复能力。

Conclusion: 施加哈密顿结构约束为高噪声条件下的医学逆问题提供了稳健的几何保障，是防止弱信号丢失的有效策略。

Abstract: Low-count Positron Emission Tomography (PET) reconstruction is severely hindered by the dissipative nature of prevailing generative models, where the inherent phase-space contraction leads to the numerical extinction (``wash-out'') of weak but diagnostically critical lesion signals. To overcome this geometric limitation, we propose \textbf{FlowPET}, a physics-informed framework that reformulates reconstruction as volume-preserving transport in a symplectic phase space. By parameterizing the posterior dynamics via a Separable Hamiltonian System, our approach guarantees a divergence-free vector field by construction, theoretically immunizing weak signals against probability mass collapse. To steer this conservative flow, we introduce conjugate boundary conditions based on the Range-Null space decomposition of the PET operator; this strictly enforces data consistency in the range space while confining stochastic uncertainty injection to the unobserved null space. We train the model via symplectic flow matching and perform inference using a symplectic leapfrog integrator. Extensive experiments on BrainWeb, clinical pediatric, and UDPET datasets demonstrate that \textbf{FlowPET} not only surpasses state-of-the-art deterministic and stochastic baselines in SSIM and PSNR but, more crucially, exhibits superior recovery of low-contrast lesions. The results confirm that imposing Hamiltonian structural constraints offers a robust geometric safeguard for medical inverse problems in high-noise regimes.

</details>


### [129] [Beyond the Eye: Efficient Multimodal Reasoning via Self-Regulated Implicit Visual Tools](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.11106)
*Xiuwei Chen,Quanlin Chen,Wentao Hu,Zisheng Chen,Kun Xiang,Zehua Ma,Mingyang Zhang,Jianhua Han,Hanhui Li,Hang Xu,Xiaodan Liang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出Beyond the Eye (BEE)范式，通过将视觉工具调用行为融入训练目标并引入自调节奖励机制，解决了多模态大模型在细粒度感知任务中依赖外部工具导致的高计算开销和推理延迟问题，在保持性能的同时显著提升推理效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有"Thinking with Images"范式在细粒度感知任务中过度依赖频繁的外部工具调用和重复图像编码，导致巨大的计算开销和推理延迟，亟需一种更高效的隐式工具调用机制。

Method: 两阶段训练：(1)形式化CoT监督微调，构建带有结构化工具槽和混合调用状态的CoT轨迹，激活隐式工具表征和自适应切换能力；(2)自调节奖励对齐，引入净工具增益(NTG)指标量化冗余工具使用，并设计自调节奖励机制惩罚无效工具依赖、鼓励知识路由。

Result: 在细粒度视觉感知任务上达到SOTA性能，在通用推理任务上保持竞争力，并在推理效率上取得显著提升。

Conclusion: BEE通过自调节的隐式工具范式，有效平衡了模型内部知识与隐式工具的使用，避免了冗余工具调用，实现了性能与效率的双赢。

Abstract: Recent multimodal large language models (MLLMs) have made remarkable progress on fine-grained perception tasks under the "Thinking with Images" (TwI) paradigm by iteratively performing various visual tool operations. However, this paradigm relies heavily on frequent external tool calls and repeated image re-encoding, which leads to substantial computational overhead and inference latency. To address these issues, we propose Beyond the Eye (BEE), a novel implicit visual tool paradigm centered on self-regulated capability. BEE directly incorporates visual tool invocation behaviors into the training objective and encourages the model to develop a self-regulated invocation mechanism. This design enables the model to adaptively balance internal knowledge and implicit tools, avoiding redundant tool usage while substantially reducing inference latency. Specifically, BEE involves a two-stage training process: (1) Formalized Chain-of-Thought (CoT) Supervised Fine-tuning (SFT). We construct CoT trajectories with structured tool slots and mixed invocation states. This stage activates the model's implicit tool representations and adaptive switching capability. (2) Self-regulated Reward-Driven Alignment. To address redundant tool usage caused by ambiguous cognitive boundaries, we first introduce the Net Tool Gain (NTG) metric to quantify this phenomenon. Based on this observation, we further propose a self-regulated reward mechanism. This mechanism penalizes ineffective tool dependency and encourages the model to perform knowledge routing, ensuring that implicit tools are invoked only when the model's internal knowledge is insufficient. BEE achieves state-of-the-art performance in fine-grained visual perception while remaining competitive in general reasoning tasks and achieving substantial gains in inference efficiency.

</details>


### [130] [GHOST: Geometry-Guided Hallucination of Opaque Surface Textures](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.11118)
*Langxu Zhao,Zuan Gu,Tianhan Gao*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出GHOST框架，利用视觉基础模型将透明物体区域转换为不透明、结构一致的表示，无需重新训练下游模型，显著提升深度估计与3D重建精度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 透明物体违反朗伯体假设，导致深度估计和3D重建中出现严重的几何退化，现有方法难以有效处理。

Method: (1) TransDINO和TransDecomp解耦掩码与透明度物理属性；(2) DAF-Net恢复表面法线先验以编码几何曲率；(3) GeoSemTransNet融合多模态线索合成保留3D结构的纹理丰富的不透明RGB图像。

Result: 在透明物体上显著提升了最先进深度估计与重建模型的精度，有效恢复了关键光度线索。

Conclusion: GHOST框架通过几何引导的预处理策略，无需模型重训练即可解决透明物体几何退化问题，具有实用性和泛化性。

Abstract: Transparent objects pose a fundamental challenge for depth estimation and 3D reconstruction due to their violation of Lambertian assumptions, leading to severe geometry degradation in downstream tasks. To address this, we propose a novel geometry-guided preprocessing framework \textbf{GHOST} that leverages visual foundation models to transform transparent regions into opaque, structurally consistent representations without requiring downstream model retraining. Specifically, our pipeline utilizes (1) \textbf{TransDINO} and (2) \textbf{TransDecomp} to disentangle masks and transparency physical properties, while (3) \textbf{DAF-Net} recovers surface normal priors to encode geometric curvature. Subsequently, (4) \textbf{GeoSemTransNet} integrates these multi-modal cues to synthesize a texture-rich opaque RGB image that preserves the transparent object's 3D structure. Extensive experiments demonstrate that our method significantly enhances the accuracy of state-of-the-art depth estimation and reconstruction models on transparent objects by restoring essential photometric cues.

</details>


### [131] [SISA-Rec: A Semantically Integrated Sequential Recommender with Contrastive Alignment](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.11168)
*Soohan Abbasi,Shahid Munir Shah,Rafia Shaikh,Mahmoud Aljawarneh*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出SISA-Rec框架，通过门控融合模块将物品ID嵌入与BERT文本嵌入结合，在自注意力机制中注入语义相似度，并利用注意力聚合模块构建用户表示，在稀疏和冷启动场景下显著优于现有模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于Transformer的序列推荐主要依赖物品ID，未能充分利用物品的语义信息，这在交互数据稀疏和冷启动场景下成为主要挑战。

Method: 采用门控融合模块融合物品ID嵌入与BERT文本嵌入；在自注意力机制中注入语义相似度；使用注意力聚合模块构建用户表示；采用BPR和对比对齐损失的联合学习目标。

Result: 在Amazon Beauty上HR@10提升16.6%，NDCG@10提升10.3%；在Amazon Toys & Games上HR@10提升23.1%，NDCG@10提升17.9%；冷启动用户改进最大。

Conclusion: 将语义信息集成到注意力机制中，能够在行为数据稀缺时提供更准确可靠的推荐，验证了语义信息在稀疏场景下的重要价值。

Abstract: Recommendation systems help users recommend relevant items from a large collection of choices. Present work on transformer-based sequential recommendation learns user preferences from interaction logs, but it mostly focuses on item identifiers and doesn't fully use the semantic meaning of items. This limitation becomes a major challenge in sparse and cold-start scenarios where historical interaction data is limited. To solve this problem, we introduce SISA-Rec (Semantically Integrated Sequential Recommendation), a transformer-based framework that embeds semantic context directly into sequential modeling. Our approach fuses item ID embeddings with BERT-based text embeddings via a gated fusion module, injects semantic similarity into the self-attention mechanism, and leverages an attention-based aggregation module to construct comprehensive user representations. Finally, a joint learning objective which combines Bayesian Personalized Ranking (BPR) and contrastive alignment loss, aligns the underlying behavioral and semantic spaces. Experiments were conducted on the two highly sparse Amazon Beauty and Amazon Toys \& Games datasets, both having 99.93\% sparsity. The results show that SISA-Rec outperforms state-of-the-art baseline models across all evaluation metrics. Compared with the BERT4Rec \cite{petrov2022systematic}, SISA-Rec improves HR@10 by 16.6\% and NDCG@10 by 10.3\% on Amazon Beauty, and HR@10 by 23.1\% and NDCG@10 by 17.9\% on Amazon Toys \& Games. Cold-start analysis further shows that the proposed model achieves the largest improvements for users with limited interaction historical records. This showcases the value of semantic information when user behavior data is scarce. Overall, the results demonstrate that integrating semantic information into the attention mechanism leads to more accurate and reliable recommendations.

</details>


### [132] [TC-MAF: Train-Calibrated Bounded Multi-Evidence Fusion for Multimodal Industrial Anomaly Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.11170)
*Ming Deng,Sijin Sun,Xiaochuan Hu,Xing Wu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出TC-MAF，一种基于锚点的多模态融合框架，结合RGB和3D证据进行异常检测，在MVTec-3D数据集上达到SOTA性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法依赖RGB重构辅助，但不同类别下可靠性不一致且缺乏类别级策略选择，需要更稳健的融合方案。

Method: 提出TC-MAF，采用基锚多证据融合设计，结合多模态检测器、Dinomaly证据和跨模态一致性线索，并引入轻量级训练分散置信度（TDC）项来调节辅助参与度。

Result: MVTec-3D上图像级AUROC达0.979，像素级AUPRO达0.990，在检测和定位上均为最佳；消融实验表明融合结构是主导因素，TDC提供可复现的校准增益。

Conclusion: TC-MAF在多种设置（少样本、缺失3D、跨数据集等）下均保持有效性，代码已开源。

Abstract: Multimodal anomaly detection benefits from complementary RGB and 3D evidence, yet auxiliary RGB reconstruction is not equally reliable across product categories and class-wise test-time policy selection is usually unavailable. We propose TC-MAF, a base-anchored multi-evidence fusion design that combines a multimodal detector, complementary Dinomaly evidence, and a small cross-modal consistency cue under one fixed pixel-level fusion formula. A lightweight training-dispersion confidence (TDC) term scales auxiliary participation using only normal training statistics. On MVTec-3D, TC-MAF reaches 0.979 image-level AUROC and 0.990 pixel-level AUPRO, achieving the best mean results on both detection and localization among the compared multimodal methods. Systematic ablations show that the fusion structure itself is the dominant factor, while TDC provides a smaller but reproducible calibration gain over no calibration or arbitrary calibration. Additional experiments show that the same design remains effective under a pooled-statistics variant, auxiliary-branch and backbone substitutions, few-shot settings, a missing-3D setting, and cross-dataset evaluation on Eyecandies. Code is available at https://anonymous.4open.science/r/TC_MAF-C3BB.

</details>


### [133] [When Depth Is Better Told Than Shown: Depth-Ordinal Prompting for Vision-Language Spatial Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.11173)
*Quynh Vo,Phuc Dao,Cong-Duy Nguyen,Thong Nguyen*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文发现给VLM输入深度图反而会降低空间推理性能，并提出DOP方法——将深度信息转化为针对查询目标的文本排序提示，无需训练或额外特征注入，实验表明深度信号以图像形式有害、以文本形式有益。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的VLM在空间推理（如远近、前后、3D布局）上表现不佳，直观的解决方案是提供深度图，但深度图反而可能降低性能。

Method: 提出Depth-Ordinal Prompting (DOP)，一种无需训练的方法，将单目深度估计结果转换为针对查询对象的顺序文本提示（如"A比B更近"），不添加深度图像、不训练模块、不注入特征、不使用标签。

Result: 在多个基准、模型和深度估计器上，DOP在伪深度提供可靠对象级排序时提升空间推理性能，在强原始图像场景下保持中性；与最强的无训练深度提示方法相比具有竞争力，同时更简单且更具针对性。

Conclusion: 深度信号的形式依赖性是关键发现：以图像形式呈现会损害性能，但以文本形式提示则有助于空间推理。

Abstract: Vision-language models (VLMs) are expected to reason about physical space -- which object is closer, what lies behind what, and how objects are arranged in 3D -- yet they still struggle with such spatial judgments. A natural remedy is to show the model a depth map, but we find that this can make performance worse. We show that depth is not absent: it reaches the language model, but becomes difficult to access for downstream reasoning, while rendered pseudo-depth maps act as noisy auxiliary images that frozen VLMs cannot easily regulate. We propose Depth-Ordinal Prompting (DOP), a training-free method that converts monocular depth into a single question-targeted ordinal text cue at the queried objects, without adding a depth image, training a module, injecting features, or using labels. Our key finding is form dependence: the same depth signal can hurt when shown as an image but help when told as text.Across benchmarks, models, and depth estimators, DOP improves spatial reasoning when pseudo-depth provides reliable object-level ordering and remains largely neutral in strong original-image regimes. It is also competitive with the strongest training-free depth-prompting alternative while being simpler and more targeted.

</details>


### [134] [GDP.pdf: Benchmarking Grounded Multimodal Reasoning over Professional PDF Documents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.11192)
*Suhaas Garre,Emily Ritchie,Sushant Mehta,Edwin Chen*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: GDP.pdf是一个由专业人士创建的PDF文档AI基准测试，包含10个领域的100个问题-文档对。测试了7个前沿模型，最佳模型通过率仅15%，最差1%。主要错误源于表格错位、图表误读、脚注遗漏等常见问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有文档AI基准测试将OCR、布局分析、图表推理、表格问答、文档VQA等能力分开评估，高分不代表模型能回答专业人士对PDF文档提出的实际复杂问题。缺乏一个直接衡量模型在实际PDF场景中表现的综合基准。

Method: GDP.pdf由10个领域的专业人士编写问题-文档对。候选问题必须至少被两个前沿多模态模型以实质性方式（错误答案、遗漏关键证据、捏造内容）答错才保留。每个项目附有原子标准的评分表，可给出分级评分和严格通过率，并按11种能力维度（文本提取与定位、表格图表理解、交叉引用、空间推理、拒绝回答等）进行标注。

Result: 在7个前沿模型（GPT-4o、Claude等）的100项测试中，最佳模型仅通过15%，最差仅通过1%。主要错误模式包括：表格错位、图表误读、脚注和除外条款遗漏、楼层平面图符号计数错误、扫描噪点干扰、修正条款覆盖原文等。

Conclusion: 当前最先进的多模态模型在处理专业PDF文档的实际问题时表现极差，存在系统性缺陷。基准测试公开可用，旨在推动文档AI能力的实质性提升。

Abstract: A large share of day-to-day work in professional domains happens inside PDF files: benefits packets, leases, datasheets, clinical guidelines, construction plans. Benchmarks for document AI have generally measured the required capabilities in isolation: OCR, layout analysis, chart reasoning, table QA, document VQA. A high score on any one of them does not necessarily reveal whether a model can answer a realistic question that someone in the field would actually ask about a specific PDF. GDP.pdf is a benchmark built to measure this directly. It consists of question-document pairs authored by working professionals in ten fields, and a candidate question was kept only when at least two frontier multimodal models failed it in a way that mattered: a wrong answer, missed decisive evidence, or a fabricated claim, rather than a superficial difference such as style. Each item comes with a rubric of atomic criteria, so we can report a graded rubric score as well as a strict task-level pass rate, and each item is tagged against a taxonomy of eleven capabilities in three tiers, spanning text extraction and grounding, table and chart comprehension, cross-referencing, spatial reasoning, and abstention on unsupported queries. We evaluated seven frontier models on the 100-item benchmark. The best model passed only 15% of the items and the worst passed 1%. Most errors trace back to a small set of recurring loss patterns: misaligned tables, misread charts, skipped footnotes and exclusions, miscounted floor-plan symbols, scan noise, and amendments that supersede earlier text. The full 100-item benchmark is publicly available at https://huggingface.co/datasets/surgeai/GDP.pdf

</details>


### [135] [Slot-RAE: Streamlining Object-Centric Learning via Direct Representation Auto-Encoders](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.11196)
*Alexandre Chapin,Emmanuel Dellandrea,Liming Chen*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出Slot-RAE，一种直接在视觉基础模型（如DINOv3）连续语义特征空间上运行的全集成框架，通过特征空间扩散和Slot Attention实现无需VAE瓶颈和预训练先验的高效对象中心场景理解与生成。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于扩散的对象中心模型几乎都依赖繁重的预训练生成先验（如Stable Diffusion）和外部VAE潜空间，导致流程复杂、计算开销大，且难以同时实现鲁棒的场景分解和高保真生成。

Method: 直接在冻结的VFM特征空间中使用Slot Attention进行对象分解，并采用Diffusion Transformer（DiT）作为解码器进行特征空间扩散，同时引入Representation Alignment（REPA）头对齐特征表示。

Result: 在COCO数据集上实现了与现有方法相当的无监督对象发现、更高质量的图像重建、稳健的零样本组合能力，同时在速度和计算效率上显著优于现有的对象中心潜扩散模型。

Conclusion: Slot-RAE通过消除对VAE瓶颈和生成预训练的依赖，以更简洁的架构在对象中心场景理解任务上达到了最优性能，为构建更高效、更轻量的场景理解框架提供了新思路。

Abstract: Deploying object-centric models for real-world scene understanding typically requires complex pipelines to achieve both robust scene decomposition and high-fidelity generation. Recent diffusion-based approaches have improved visual quality, but they almost universally rely on heavy, pretrained generative priors (e.g., Stable Diffusion) and external VAE latent spaces. In this paper, we propose Slot-RAE, a much simpler, fully integrated framework that operates directly within the continuous semantic feature space of visual foundation models (e.g., DINOv3). Slot-RAE employs a feature-space diffusion process using a Diffusion Transformer (DiT) decoder and a Representation Alignment (REPA) head. Unlike existing diffusion-based objectcentric methods that rely heavily on subsidized text-toimage priors, the generative core of Slot-RAE (Slot Attention and the DiT) is trained from scratch within the frozen VFM feature space. This eliminates the need for VAE bottlenecks and task-agnostic generative pre-training. Experiments on the COCO dataset demonstrate that despite its architectural simplicity, Slot-RAE achieves state-of-the-art results. It delivers comparable unsupervised object discovery, higher-fidelity image reconstruction, and robust zero-shot compositionality, all while being significantly faster and more computationally efficient than existing object-centric latent diffusion models.

</details>


### [136] [DynEval: Holistic Evaluations of T2I Generative Models in the Wild](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.11199)
*Shyam Marjit,Dheeraj Baiju,Anuj Shikarkhane,Akhil Sakthieswaran,Sayak Paul,Anirban Chakraborty*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出DynEval，一个用于动态评估文生图模型的框架，通过构建大规模数据集GenDB和DynEvalInstruct，并采用课程学习策略微调出DynEval-2B/4B评估器，在11个基准上取得了与人类判断更一致的结果，并能对36个T2I模型进行细粒度分析。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有自动评估方法依赖静态提示集，难以捕捉部分提示不对齐、组合错误、语义正确但视觉合理等失败模式，且缺乏可扩展的高质量评估数据。

Method: 构建GenDB（50万提示-图像对）和DynEvalInstruct（25万指令数据集），采用分层提示-模型生成策略和结构化评估流水线，通过课程学习对大视觉语言模型进行全参数微调，得到DynEval-2B和DynEval-4B评估器。

Result: 在11个基准上与人类判断的相关性优于现有评估器，并对36个T2I模型在42个子类别和9个语义维度上进行了细粒度分析。

Conclusion: DynEval通过动态评估框架和大规模数据构建，有效提升了文生图模型的评估能力，能够捕捉细粒度的失败模式，具有良好的可扩展性和实用性。

Abstract: Recent advances in text-to-image (T2I) generation have led to models capable of producing highly realistic images. Yet, reliably evaluating their outputs remains challenging, especially at scale. Existing automatic evaluators, often relying on a static prompt set, struggle to capture subtle failure modes such as partial prompt misalignment, compositional errors, or visually plausible but semantically incorrect generations. In this work, we introduce DynEval, a Dynamic Evaluation framework designed to jointly assess text-to-image alignment and image quality of T2I models. To support scalable training beyond limited human-annotated data, we construct two large datasets. First, we build GenDB, a collection of 500K prompt-image pairs generated from human-written prompts drawn from DiffusionDB using a tiered prompt-model generation strategy. Second, building upon GenDB, we construct DynEvalInstruct, a 250K instruction dataset comprising prompt-image-response triplets distilled from a structured evaluation pipeline that decomposes evaluation into text-image alignment and visual quality reasoning. Using this dataset, we perform full fine-tuning of a compact evaluator through a curriculum learning strategy to effectively distill the superior evaluation capabilities of a larger teacher vision-language model, resulting in DynEval-2B and DynEval-4B. In extensive comparisons against existing evaluators across 11 benchmarks, our evaluator achieves a higher overall correlation with human judgments. Furthermore, it provides fine-grained analysis of the capabilities and failure modes of 36 T2I models across 42 subcategories and 9 semantic dimensions.

</details>


### [137] [Parallax Portrait Matting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.11205)
*Xin Cai,Jiawen Chen,Lars Jebe,Tianfan Xue,Zhoutong Zhang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出Parallax Portrait Matting方法，利用连拍照片中的微小视差变化（双帧图像）来改善人像抠图效果，无需绿幕或偏振光等特殊设备，在纹理复杂场景下显著优于单图像抠图方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 单图像抠图在前后景纹理丰富时高度病态（ill-posed），现有方法虽能通过绿幕、偏振光等额外信号提升效果，但依赖特殊拍摄设备，缺乏实用性。

Method: 利用连拍中微小相机运动产生的视差，估计trimap和前后景运动，构建对齐视图；网络通过背景对齐图像直接融合，并通过交叉注意力机制利用前景对齐线索补偿运动估计误差。

Result: 在挑战性人像案例上，相比强单图像抠图基线，本方法恢复了更精细的细节和更准确的前景颜色。

Conclusion: 双帧视差信息能有效解决单图像抠图的病态问题，为实用化高精度抠图提供新思路。

Abstract: Image matting is highly ill-posed, especially when both the foreground and background are richly textured. While single-image matting methods learn strong priors from data, they often struggle on these challenging cases. Existing approaches improve results by requiring additional signals such as green screens, polarized lighting, or clean background images, but these typically rely on specialized capture setups. We present Parallax Portrait Matting, a practical two-frame matting method that uses a second image captured with slight viewpoint change. Such a setting arises naturally in burst photography, where small camera motion induces foreground-background parallax and provides complementary observations for matting. Our pipeline estimates trimaps and foreground/background motion, then constructs aligned views for prediction. To handle imperfect motion estimation, the network uses the background-aligned pair for direct fusion and the foreground-aligned cue through cross-attention for error compensation. Experiments show that our method recovers finer details and more accurate foreground colors than strong single-image matting baselines on challenging portrait cases.

</details>


### [138] [A Novel Method to Evaluate Models on Unreliable, Noisy and Inconsistent Labels: Adaptive Resolution Label Aggregation (ARLA)](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.11214)
*Natasha Randall,Gernot Heisenberg*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出了一种名为ARLA的自适应分辨率标签聚合方法，在推理时动态调整标签和模型预测的分辨率，以解决分割模型评估中标签边界不一致、噪声或模糊的问题，并在洪水预测模型中验证了其有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法主要关注在弱标签上训练模型，但缺乏在不可靠标签上评估模型的有效方法，因此需要一种能在标签存在噪声、不一致或模糊时仍能准确评估模型性能的技术。

Method: ARLA（Adaptive Resolution Label Aggregation）方法：在推理时动态调整标签和模型预测的分辨率，然后在调整后的分辨率上计算评估指标。

Result: 在真实洪水预测模型中，ARLA成功克服了森林区域标签不一致以及重云覆盖区域标签错误的问题，能够从噪声中提取更清晰的模型真实性能信号。

Conclusion: ARLA提供了一种新的分割模型评估方法，具有可调参数以适应标签精度或噪声水平，能够最小化标签误差并更准确地反映模型真实表现。

Abstract: Labels are critical for both training and evaluating deep learning segmentation models, but are often inconsistent, noisy, or ambiguous at class boundaries. Many approaches have been developed to support training models on weak labels, but few to none currently exist to facilitate evaluating models on unreliable labels. We therefore introduce a method called "Adaptive Resolution Label Aggregation", or "ARLA", which dynamically adapts the resolution of both the label and the model prediction at inference time before the evaluation metrics are computed. We demonstrate how ARLA can be used to better analyse model behaviour with a practical application to a real flood prediction model, where ARLA was able to overcome issues with inconsistent labelling of forested areas and errors in labels within regions of heavy cloud cover. Our work presents a new approach to evaluating segmentation models, with adjustable parameters to adapt the aggregated resolution to the precision of the label or the level of label noise. Fundamentally, ARLA exploits the information encapsulated by a label but minimises the label error, extracting from the noise a clearer signal of a model's true performance.

</details>


### [139] [HandFlow: Fully Generative 4D Hand Recovery with Flow Matching](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.11221)
*Mingxi Xu,Bowen Duan,Yi Gu,Zhengyang Shen,Renjing Xu,Yutao Yue*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: HandFlow 是一个基于流匹配的生成式框架，用于从单目视频中实现时空一致的手部姿态和形状估计，通过单次 ODE 积分去噪整个时间窗口的 MANO 参数，在 DexYCB 和 HOT3D 上达到 SOTA，世界空间误差降低超 30%，处理速度达 47 fps，比现有最快视频方法快约 12 倍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有单目 4D 手部重建方法存在两大问题：逐帧判别式回归器缺乏时序上下文，预测结果抖动明显；时序模型虽能聚合帧间信息，但仍是确定性回归器，在遮挡和运动模糊等模糊观测下表现脆弱。生成式建模通过学习合理手部运动序列的先验，能在视觉证据不完整时恢复连贯的手部状态，但此前缺乏高效的生成式时序框架。

Method: 提出 HandFlow 框架，采用流匹配生成范式：给定视觉和骨骼观测，通过单次 ODE 积分对整个时间窗口的 MANO 参数进行去噪。核心组件包括：(1) Flux 风格的双流 Transformer，在全序列上捕获长程依赖，无需自回归解码；(2) 置信度感知的连续掩码机制，将观测特征与可学习的掩码标记融合，以处理噪声或缺失的观测数据。

Result: 在 DexYCB 和 HOT3D 上达到 SOTA：世界空间位姿误差相比最强基线降低超 30%，加速度误差在所有评估方法中最低，同时在逐帧位姿精度上保持竞争力。在单 GPU 上，HandFlow 能以 47 fps 重建 150 帧序列，速度比现有最快视频方法快约 12 倍。

Conclusion: HandFlow 证明了生成式流匹配框架在单目视频 4D 手部重建中的有效性，兼具高精度、强时序一致性和实时推理速度，为处理遮挡和运动模糊等挑战性场景提供了新思路。

Abstract: Accurate monocular 4D hand reconstruction remains challenging. Per-frame discriminative regressors lack temporal context and often produce jittery predictions. Temporal models improve consistency by aggregating information across frames, but they are typically deterministic regressors, making them vulnerable to ambiguous observations caused by occlusion and motion blur. Generative modeling offers a natural alternative by learning a prior over plausible hand motion sequences, enabling coherent hand-state recovery when visual evidence is incomplete or unreliable.
  Motivated by this observation, we present HandFlow, a fully generative flow-matching framework for temporally coherent 3D hand pose and shape estimation from monocular video. Given visual and skeletal observations, HandFlow denoises an entire temporal window of MANO parameters through a single ODE integration. To support this, we use a Flux-style dual-stream transformer that attends across the full sequence to capture long-range dependencies without autoregressive decoding, and a confidence-aware continuous masking mechanism that blends observed features with learnable mask tokens to handle noisy or missing observations. Experiments on DexYCB and HOT3D show that HandFlow achieves state-of-the-art performance, with particularly large gains in world-space accuracy and temporal smoothness. It reduces world-space pose error by over 30% compared with the strongest baseline and achieves the lowest acceleration error among all evaluated methods, while remaining competitive in per-frame pose accuracy. Moreover, on a single GPU HandFlow reconstructs a 150-frame sequence at 47 fps, about 12x faster than the fastest prior video-based method, with reconstruction itself accounting for only a small fraction of the end-to-end latency.

</details>


### [140] [Structure-Detail Decoupled Autoregressive Generation for Fast and High-Fidelity Virtual Try-On](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.11233)
*Lu Yang,Xiaonan Hu,Yanan Li,Daqi Liu,Xiang Bai,Hao Lu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出STAR-VTON，一种两阶段自回归虚拟试穿框架，将潜在空间的结构合成与像素空间的细节恢复解耦，在保证效率的同时显著提升保真度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 扩散VTON方法虽能建模试穿，但存在高频细节丢失和推理成本高的问题；VAR模型虽高效，但缺乏有效的双条件机制，且潜在空间生成仍难以保留细粒度服装细节。

Method: 首先构建VAR-VTON作为VAR基线的VTON模型；然后提出STAR-VTON，将任务分解为两阶段：第一阶段在潜在空间进行结构合成（服装变形、人物布局），第二阶段在像素空间通过匹配引导的细化器恢复细粒度细节。

Result: VAR-VTON比扩散方法快4倍以上且质量不降；像素空间细化器有效恢复细节，可作为即插即用模块提升现有VTON方法。

Conclusion: 不同VTON子任务应分别在不同表征空间处理，这种解耦策略实现了效率与保真度的出色平衡。

Abstract: Virtual try-on (VTON) is a bi-conditional image generation problem that requires not only accurate person preservation but also faithful garment deformation and detail synthesis. Diffusion-based VTON methods can jointly model these factors in a compressed latent space, but suffer from high-frequency detail loss due to inherent latent compression, even with costly multi-step denoising. Recent visual autoregressive (VAR) models offer a promising alternative for high-quality generation with faster inference, yet remain unexplored for VTON due to the lack of effective bi-conditioning mechanisms. To bridge this gap, we first introduce VAR-VTON, a VAR-based VTON model that incorporates garment conditioning and structural guidance for efficient latent-space VTON. Despite its efficacy, latent-space generation still struggles to preserve fine-grained garment details. We argue that different VTON sub-tasks should be addressed in different representation spaces: structural synthesis such as garment warping and person layout is suited to the latent space, whereas fine-grained detail recovery should be tackled in the pixel space. Motivated by this insight, we further propose STAR-VTON, a Two-Stage AutoRegressive framework that builds upon VAR-VTON by decoupling latent-space structural synthesis from pixel-space detail recovery. Our idea is to resort to a matching-informed refiner to establish dense correspondences between the stage-one generation and the source garment to directly map fine-grained pixel-space details. Extensive experiments show that STAR-VTON achieves an impressive efficiency-fidelity trade-off: VAR-VTON runs at least $4\times$ faster than diffusion-based counterparts without degrading quality, and the pixel-space refiner effectively restores fine details and acts as a plug-and-play module that can benefit existing VTON approaches.

</details>


### [141] [A Nearable Soft Mat Based on Distributed Optical Fiber Sensing for Physiological Monitoring](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.11255)
*Vincenzo Lavorgna,Martina Pulcinelli,Andrea Polimadei,Rosaria D Amato,Carlo Massaroni,Michele Arturo Caponero,Emiliano Schena,Daniela Lo Presti*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于分布式光纤传感（DOFS）和光频域反射技术（OFDR）的柔软传感垫，用于大面积生理监测。通过将单模光纤嵌入硅胶基质并设计蛇形布局，实现了2250个传感点，可高空间分辨率监测心率和呼吸频率。在6名志愿者座椅背靠实验中，该系统成功实现了二维映射和生理参数估计，验证了其在近身可穿戴健康监测中的可行性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管OFDR基DOFS具有高空间分辨率优势，但在生物医学应用中尚未得到充分探索，而临床上迫切需要灵敏、空间可分辨且贴合舒适的传感界面，因此本研究旨在填补这一空白。

Method: 将单模光纤嵌入柔性硅胶基质，采用蛇形布局分布传感区域，栅距2.6 mm，共2250个传感点，采样频率50 Hz；在6名健康志愿者座椅靠背的近身配置下进行实验验证。

Result: 系统成功实现了二维响应映射，反映出机械耦合和心肺诱导的扰动；估计的呼吸率和心率与参考可穿戴系统结果高度一致，时空模式具有生理学一致性。

Conclusion: 证明了将大面积分布式传感、空间映射与定量心肺监测整合于DOFS基柔软近身界面的可行性，为未来生物医学应用提供了新途径。

Abstract: Distributed optical fiber sensing (DOFS) combines the advantages of fiber optic sensors, including flexibility, small size, immunity to electromagnetic interference, and high metrological performance, with the capability to transform a single optical fiber into a continuous sensing element for spatially resolved mechanical measurements. Optical frequency domain reflectometry (OFDR), based on Rayleigh backscattering, enables high spatial resolution DOFS measurements, broadening the range of potential sensing applications. However, OFDR based DOFS remains largely unexplored for biomedical applications, despite the need for sensitive, spatially resolved, and conformable sensing interfaces. This study presents a soft DOFS based mat as a large-area interface for physiological monitoring. A single-mode optical fiber was embedded in a flexible silicone matrix and arranged in a serpentine layout to distribute sensing over the mat surface. With a gage pitch of 2.6 mm, the system provided 2250 sensing sites across the active area at a sampling frequency of 50 Hz. The mat was assessed on six healthy volunteers in a seated nearable configuration on the backrest of a standard office chair. The distributed output enabled two dimensional mapping of the mat response, reflecting back mat mechanical coupling and cardiorespiratory induced perturbations. Respiratory rate and heart rate were therefore estimated and compared with a reference wearable system. The maps revealed physiologically coherent spatial and temporal patterns, while the estimated rates showed good agreement with the reference measurements. These results demonstrate the feasibility of combining large area distributed sensing, spatial mapping, and quantitative cardiorespiratory monitoring within a DOFS based soft nearable interface.

</details>


### [142] [LaGuadia: Language-Guided Adaptive Distillation from Pathology Foundation Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.11257)
*Gangsu Kim,Won-Ki Jeong*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出LaGuadia框架，通过临床语言引导的自适应知识蒸馏，将多个病理基础模型的知识压缩至87M参数的小模型，性能媲美GigaPath等大模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 病理基础模型计算成本高昂，现有知识蒸馏方法采用均匀加权，忽略了组织异质性，无法有效整合多个教师模型的知识。

Method: 多阶段流水线：1)从病理报告中提取视觉可观察的临床关键词；2)用MedSigLIP视觉-语言元教师将视觉特征与关键词对齐；3)基于语义对齐程度自适应加权教师贡献进行知识蒸馏。

Result: 87M参数的LaGuadia学生模型在WSI字幕生成、视觉问答和切片分类任务上匹配或超越GigaPath、UNI等大规模模型，具备强事实一致性和鲁棒泛化能力。

Conclusion: 临床语言可作为有效的语义锚点，用于构建高效可靠的数字病理学系统。

Abstract: Pathology Foundation Models (PFMs) offer powerful Whole Slide Image (WSI) representations but suffer from massive computational costs. While Knowledge Distillation (KD) can create efficient student models, existing multi-teacher methods often use suboptimal uniform weighting that ignores tissue heterogeneity. We propose LaGuadia (Language-Guided Adaptive DistillAtion), a framework that develops a compact pathology image encoder by dynamically integrating expertise from multiple PFMs under clinical linguistic guidance. Our approach utilizes a multi-stage pipeline: first, extracting visually observable clinical keywords from pathology reports; second, aligning visual features with these keywords via a Vision-Language meta-teacher (MedSigLIP) to provide dense semantic guidance; and finally, performing adaptive KD where teacher contributions are weighted based on their semantic alignment with the clinical narrative. Experiments on WSI captioning, visual question answering, and slide-level classification tasks demonstrate that an 87M parameter LaGuadia student model matches or exceeds foundation-scale models such as GigaPath and UNI, achieving strong factual consistency and robust generalization. These results highlight clinical language as an effective semantic anchor for building efficient and reliable digital pathology systems. Code is available at https://github.com/hvcl/LaGuadia.

</details>


### [143] [The Devil Is in the Leakage: A Disentangled Dual-Purification Framework for High-Fidelity Hairstyle Transfer](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.11281)
*Jijie Li,Jiankuo Zhao,Xiangyu Zhu,Zhen Lei*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出双净化框架（DPF），针对发型迁移中身份泄露和瑕疵泄露两大问题，通过对抗性发型净化（AHP）和对比几何净化（CGP）两种正则化方法，实现高质量、保身份的发型迁移。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有发型迁移方法存在两大问题：一是发型特征会泄露参考图像的身份/姿态信息（身份泄露）；二是"秃头"图像中的残留伪影会传递到最终合成结果（瑕疵泄露）。

Method: 提出双净化框架（DPF），包含两个训练时正则化器：1）对抗性发型净化（AHP）——基于互信息启发的对抗目标抑制发型特征中的身份可预测性；2）对比几何净化（CGP）——通过对比学习目标正则化ControlNet通路，减少模型对秃头条件中几何伪影的依赖。

Result: 在多个基准数据集上实现了最先进的性能，生成高保真、保身份的发型迁移结果。

Conclusion: 同时净化发型表征和几何通路，DPF有效解决了发型迁移中的双重泄露问题，显著提升了生成质量。

Abstract: Hairstyle transfer aims to synthesize a photorealistic portrait by transplanting the hairstyle from a reference image onto a source subject while preserving the source identity. Recent foundation models show strong generative capability, but they struggle with the zero-shot disentanglement required for precise local editing, often entangling the reference hairstyle with its original identity and pose. Existing diffusion-based pipelines typically decompose the task by first generating a "bald" image from the source and then injecting hairstyle features from the reference. However, we show that this paradigm suffers from a fundamental leakage problem. Identity Leakage in Hairstyle occurs when hairstyle features retain reference identity or pose information, while Flaw Leakage in Bald arises when residual artifacts in the bald image are propagated into the final synthesis. To address both issues, we propose the Dual-Purification Framework (DPF), which introduces two complementary training-time regularizers. Adversarial Hairstyle Purification (AHP) purifies hairstyle features by suppressing identity predictability under a mutual-information-inspired adversarial objective. Contrastive Geometric Purification (CGP) regularizes the ControlNet pathway with a contrastive objective, reducing the model's reliance on geometric artifacts in the bald condition. By jointly purifying the hairstyle representation and geometric pathway, DPF achieves high-fidelity, identity-preserving hairstyle transfer and state-of-the-art performance on diverse benchmarks.

</details>


### [144] [SalientGS: Unified SfM-to-3DGS with Importance-Guided MCMC Gaussian Allocation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.11285)
*Tianyu Xiong,Rui Li,Suning Ge,Jiaqi Yang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: SalientGS提出了一种无需SfM预处理的端到端3D高斯泼溅管线，通过重要性引导的MCMC高斯分配，在15分钟内实现高质量3D重建。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统3D场景重建依赖昂贵的SfM预处理和固定的姿态接口，这成为瓶颈。SalientGS旨在消除这一预处理步骤，实现统一的端到端重建管线。

Method: 核心方法是重要性引导的马尔可夫链蒙特卡洛（MCMC）高斯分配。通过聚合多视角残差，生成每个高斯的欠拟合和冗余信号，定义平滑的重要性加权采样分布，引导高斯的新生和重定位向欠拟合区域偏移，从而重新分配容量，同时不改变底层的随机梯度朗之万动力学（SGLD）。

Result: SalientGS仅需15分钟即可实现端到端重建，并取得当前最优的感知质量。

Conclusion: SalientGS成功证明了通过智能的高斯分配策略，可以在去掉SfM预处理的情况下实现高效、高质量的3D重建，代码已开源。

Abstract: Reconstructing 3D scenes from unordered images remains bottlenecked by expensive Structure-from-Motion (SfM) preprocessing and frozen pose interfaces. We present SalientGS, a unified SfM-to-3D Gaussian Splatting (3DGS) pipeline. Its central contribution is importance-guided Markov Chain Monte Carlo (MCMC) Gaussian allocation, which aggregates multi-view residuals into per-Gaussian underfit and redundancy signals. These signals define a smooth importance-weighted sampling distribution that biases both birth and relocation toward underfit regions. This reallocates capacity from well-fit areas without altering the underlying stochastic gradient Langevin dynamics (SGLD). SalientGS achieves end-to-end reconstruction in 15 minutes with state-of-the-art perceptual quality. The supplementary material provides dedicated sections for Per-Scene Qualitative Comparisons and Per-Image Learned Perceptual Image Patch Similarity (LPIPS) Analysis, including failure cases. Code and evaluation scripts are available at https://github.com/Six-Bit-TX/SalientGS.

</details>


### [145] [A Unified Framework for Comprehensive Cardiac CT Segmentation and Phenotyping: Human-in-the-Loop Data Annotation, Vision Foundation Model Development, Multicenter Evaluation and Clinical Validation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.11287)
*Pooya Mohammadi Kazaj,Leo Fridolin Weber,Wen Xie,Seyed Amir Ahmad Safavi-Naini,Anselm Stark,Giovanni Baj,Ali Mokhtari,Toshiya Yoshida,Christoph Ryffel,Taishi Okuno,Yoshihiro Akashi,Ronny R. Buechel,Thomas Pilgrim,Waldo Valenzuela,George C. M. Siontis,Xiaowei Xu,Moritz Hundertmark,Stephan Windecker,Christoph Grani,Isaac Shiri*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出了一个统一的CT心脏分割与表型分析框架，结合人工标注流程、数据增强技术和基于6万例无标注数据预训练的自监督基础模型，构建了迄今最大最全面的专家标注心脏CT分割数据集（1598例，14个结构），在5个外部数据集上表现优于现有工具，并实现了群体层面表型分析。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 心脏CT的全面量化受限于测量可扩展性而非数据可用性，无法满足常规临床使用需求。

Method: 采用人机交互标注流程、心脏CT数据增强技术，以及基于6万例无标注心脏CT扫描预训练的自监督基础模型。

Result: 构建了1598例、14个心脏结构的数据集；在5个外部数据集上分割更准确全面；自监督预训练提升标注效率；数据质量和预训练比架构更决定准确性；实现了群体水平的表型分析。

Conclusion: 通过开源最大的带标签数据集、代码、模型权重、增强库和软件，为常规CT扫描中的机会性心脏表型分析提供了可复现的基础。

Abstract: Comprehensive quantification of cardiac structures from computed tomography (CT) remains limited not by data availability but by the scalability of measurements, which makes routine use impractical. Here we present a unified framework for comprehensive cardiac CT segmentation and phenotyping that combines a human-in-the-loop annotation pipeline, a cardiac CT augmentation technique, and a self-supervised foundation model pre-trained on 60,000 unlabeled cardiac CT scans. Using this approach, we assembled the largest and most comprehensive expert-annotated cardiac CT segmentation dataset to date, comprising 1598 cases and 14 distinct cardiac structures (1000 for training, 598 for the external test set). Across five external datasets, the framework segmented all structures more accurately and comprehensively than existing open-source tools. Self-supervised pre-training improved labeling efficiency, with the most significant gains observed during external evaluation in the low-data regime. Benchmarking across convolutional, transformer, and state-space architectures showed comparable performance, indicating that data quality and pre-training, rather than architecture, drove accuracy. The framework was scaled to population-level phenotyping, with segmented anatomy that carries functionally relevant information about ventricular function and disease severity beyond demographic variables. By openly releasing the largest dataset with human labels, code, model weights, a CT augmentation library, and software, this work provides a reproducible foundation for opportunistic cardiac phenotyping from routinely acquired CT scans.

</details>


### [146] [Metadata Supervised MRI Representations for Modelling and Controlling Acquisition Variability](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.11295)
*Mehmet Yigit Avci,Pedro Borges,Virginia Fernandez,Natalia Glazman,Paul Wright,Mehmet Yigitsoy,Sebastien Ourselin,Jorge Cardoso*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出一种通过联合建模MRI图像与DICOM元数据来分离解剖结构与采集对比度信息的方法，实现了跨模态、跨站点的解剖保留图像协调，并支持采集变异的建模、审计与控制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: MRI采集存在显著变异，相同解剖结构在不同扫描仪和协议下表现差异巨大，导致学习到的表示混淆了生物结构与非目标采集依赖信息，限制了模型的可解释性、泛化能力和临床部署。

Method: 利用大规模临床脑MRI数据，联合建模图像与DICOM元数据，学习分离解剖结构表示与对比度相关表示；并基于解耦表示构建跨模态/跨站点的解剖保留协调模型，支持以图像或采集元数据为条件进行适应。

Result: 对比度表示能够组织异质采集数据、支持序列理解、检测图像与元数据不一致性；解剖表示抑制了采集特定变异，同时保留生物相关信息。

Conclusion: 采集变异性并非不可控噪声，而是成像过程中可建模、可审计、可控制的结构化组成部分，为大规模医学影像中的采集感知表示学习奠定了基础。

Abstract: Magnetic resonance imaging exhibits substantial acquisition variability, where identical anatomy can appear markedly different across scanners and imaging protocols. Consequently, learned representations entangle biological structure with acquisition-dependent appearance, limiting interpretability, generalisation, and clinical deployment. We show that these sources of variation can be separated by jointly modelling MRI images and DICOM metadata. Using large-scale clinical brain MRI data, we learn representations that separate anatomical structure from contrast-dependent appearance. Resulting contrast representations organise heterogeneous acquisitions, support sequence understanding, and detect image--metadata inconsistencies, whereas anatomical representations suppress acquisition-specific variation while preserving biologically relevant information. Building on these disentangled representations, we introduce a unified anatomy-preserving harmonisation model for cross-modality and cross-site adaptation, conditioned on image or acquisition metadata. Our findings suggest that acquisition variability is a structured component of the imaging process that can be modelled, audited, and controlled, providing a foundation for acquisition-aware representation learning in large-scale medical imaging.

</details>


### [147] [ASUMOT: Motion-Consistency-Based Asynchronous UAV Detection and Tracking with Event Cameras](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.11303)
*Baofeng Jia,Xiaoyu Chen,Jingyuan Zhang,Zongze Wu,Haochen li,Jing Han,Lianfa Bai*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出ASUMOT框架，利用运动一致性直接在原始事件流上实现异步无人机检测与跟踪，解决长距离无人机事件响应稀疏、碎片化导致的轨迹重复与身份不稳定问题，并发布ES-UAV高分辨率事件级无人机基准数据集。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 长距离无人机在事件相机中产生稀疏、碎片化且含噪声的事件响应，同一语义目标可能呈现为多个空间分离的斑块，导致直接在斑块级别进行异步跟踪会出现重复轨迹和身份不稳定问题。

Method: ASUMOT框架包含三个核心模块：局部运动一致性估计器触发可靠候选目标；轻量级多任务验证器提供无人机置信度和运动方向提示；运动一致性聚类将碎片化斑块聚合为身份一致的无人机轨迹。

Result: 在公开无人机跟踪数据和ES-UAV基准上，ASUMOT在保持异步事件处理的同时，提升了准确率与效率的权衡。

Conclusion: 运动一致性可以有效解决事件相机中长距离无人机的稀疏碎片化检测与跟踪难题，ASUMOT在精度和效率上均取得优越表现。

Abstract: Event cameras offer microsecond-level temporal resolution and high dynamic range for low-altitude UAV perception. However, long-range UAVs often produce sparse, fragmented, and noise-contaminated event responses, where one semantic target may appear as multiple spatially separated blobs. Direct blob-level asynchronous tracking therefore suffers from duplicate trajectories and unstable identities. We propose ASUMOT, a motion-consistency-based asynchronous UAV detection and tracking framework operating directly on raw events. ASUMOT models each UAV as a set of motion-consistent event blobs.
